- [x] **57.18 Functional audit FA-20261005-02 (transfer approval 405) - fixed and deployed (`c88b9e8`, 2026-10-07).** Root cause: `approveTransferOrder` posted to `/api/v1/doc/TransferOrder/<id>` unencoded; transfer numbers contain slashes, so the request hit another route (405). The same bug sat in Sticker Template edit/save, Report Filter Preset and Yard Check-In - all encoded; no unencoded record-id URL remains in `public/`. Also: the transfer line item became a real picker, Approvals shows readable record types, non-admins no longer call the admin-only industry-lock endpoint. Proven by recording the full transfer create/approve/dispatch/receive for the SOP with no API errors. (The audit file itself belongs to another session and is left unedited.)
- [x] **57.17 Roles and users, the way the user asked (raised mid-round, 2026-10-04).** "Create
  roles and assign users; a user sees that module only; user and role both required; login and
  browse only, no OTP; reset password at first login; org admin and super admin can reset a
  forgotten password; super admin can disable a user." Already true before this pass: role
  required on create; MFA only for Super Admin/HR-Admin; Deactivate (sessions die within ~30s);
  admin Reset Password. Built: `POST /api/v1/admin/roles` + a `roles` registry so a role exists
  before anyone holds it (admin names and case-duplicates refused); user create requires an
  existing role (stored in its registered spelling); grants accept `doctype_names` so the Roles
  screen grants **a whole module** in one transaction; `users.must_change_password` set on create
  and admin reset, cleared on any self-chosen password, enforced in apiMiddleware (403 +
  `X-Password-Change-Required`, only /me and change-password allowed) and read column-tolerantly
  so a binary ahead of its migration cannot take sessions down; a "Set your own password" step
  on the login card. **Menu: 18 module screens were `open: true` to every role** - replaced with a
  module rule (visible once the role can read anything in the module); `menu-returns` and
  `menu-rf-traceability` had no rule at all; Approvals is cross-module (never keeps the Finance
  flyout open alone; Home's card reaches it). Verified by API (12 checks: forced change, 403
  until changed, ungranted doctype 403, reset revokes sessions and re-forces, disabled cannot
  sign in) and in a browser (a "Store Keeper" granted Inventory sees Home, Reports, Knowledge
  Center, Stock, Setup only). Docs: ADMIN_GUIDE §B.2, USER_GUIDE §2.

**Verification for this round** (2026-10-05/06): two disposable databases on the local Postgres
(`erp_qa57_20261004` for suites, `erp_qa57_live_20261004` for the live server - the shared
`custom_erp_test` was not touched); all 171 migrations from empty, Stage 57's migration re-run
idempotent and its HSN backfill proven on seeded Items; `go build`/`vet` clean; `internal/server`,
`engines`, `internal/kb`, `cmd/*` suites green except three **pre-existing, unrelated** failures that
only appear on a freshly migrated schema (`migrations_stores_master_fields.sql` re-adds a
`Stores` doctype with module "Store"); 17/17 browser checks green with no page errors.
**Release budget:** this round first pushed both NFR-COST-001 gates over (initial JS by 51 B,
cold core by ~5.7 KB gzip); fixed by moving the picker and quick-create dialog (and their CSS)
into lazily loaded `public/view-pickers.js`, then condensing this round's own shell comments - now
passing with **~1.4 KB cold-core headroom** (`app.js` 106,513 B gzip). Thin: the next sizeable shell
addition by any session should expect to trip it.
**Also fixed this round:** (1) every page load sends ~28 CSP reports (`style-src-attr`, report-only)
with no Authorization header, which shared the IP-keyed "default" bucket (60/min) - several tills
behind one shop IP could exhaust it and get an ordinary unauthenticated request (the login screen's)
refused with a **Rate Limit** message: the likely source of the user's question. CSP reports now have
their own bucket (`rateLimitCategory`, test `TestCSPReportsHaveTheirOwnRateLimitBucket`); the inline
styles themselves remain a separate clean-up. (2) The user's SOP PO chapter was blocked because a
typeahead row sat under the fixed environment banner: menus were positioned once and never followed
their box. `placeMenu` now opens upward when needed, stays below the banner, follows on scroll and
closes when the box leaves the view; verified with the banner shown (PO item suggestion clicks).


## Stage 58 — Sticker Studio: a full label designer with exact-look printing (opened + built 2026-10-07; released 2026-10-09 in `f6e3961`; only 58.7 open)

Opened from the user's request to build sticker printing "with this sort of flexibility … best module ever and better than this", with a screenshot of another ERP's label designer (92 × 12 jewellery tag: size presets, DPI, stickers/row, gaps, roll/media, font family, zoom, snapping, multi-select, QR, rotated text). User decisions (2026-10-07): **printers = TSC (TSPL), Zebra (ZPL) and A4/laser sheets**; **text = exact look** (rendered at printer DPI, any font) — *"but don't steal the UI. Make it better/best UI possible and not similar UI"*; silent printing goes through the print agent, which may be our own PrintBridge (`../PrintBridge/agent/`, QZ wire protocol) rather than QZ Tray — no QZ-specific dependency added.

- [x] **58.1 Rendering engine** — new `public/sticker-engine.js`, the one renderer for studio canvas, thumbnails, browser print and thermal payloads. Draws every element on a canvas at the printer's DPI, thresholds to 1-bit, and emits a TSPL `BITMAP` (inverted bits, `SIZE`/`GAP`/`DIRECTION`/`DENSITY`/`SPEED`) or ZPL `^GFA` with standard ASCII compression (`^PW`/`^LL`/`^PON|^POI`/`~SD`/`^PR`/`^PQ`). Identical consecutive rows collapse into one image with a copy count; jobs go in 50-row batches (PrintBridge/QZ cap one WebSocket message at 32 MB). Browser path: PNGs at ≥300 dpi laid out as roll pages (CSS named `@page` per template, so one job mixes sizes) or an A4 sheet grid. Hand-written encoders, no library: **Code 128** (auto B/C subset — a 13-digit value is 123 modules vs 178 in pure B), **EAN-13**, **QR** (byte mode, v1-40, L/M/Q/H, mask penalty). Bars/modules are whole printer dots. **Verified**: Code 128 — 20 edge-case values decode exactly with ZXing; EAN-13 — 7 values incl. the ERP's own dated `0200…` codes decode with ZXing; QR — all 160 version × ECC combinations at max capacity + Unicode (₹, Devanagari) decode (159 with jsQR; v23-L, which jsQR alone rejects, decodes with ZXing); ZPL compression — 400 random/structured bitmaps round-trip through an independent spec decompressor.
- [x] **58.2 Sticker Studio** — new `public/sticker-studio.js` + `.stk-*` styles: full-screen workspace (own layout, not a copy): top bar (inline name, size chip, undo/redo, **Preview with** a real Item via typeahead, **Test print**, **Save**), icon **Insert** rail (Text, Data, Barcode, QR, Line, Box, Logo), searchable **Data fields** drawer (every label field + every Item field from `/api/v1/doc/Item/meta`; click or drag onto the label), stage with mm rulers, multi-up ghost labels, 1 mm/5 mm grid, smart guides + 0.5 mm grid snap (Alt bypasses), marquee and Shift/Ctrl multi-select, 8-handle resize, floating quick bar (rotate/duplicate/front/back/lock/delete) parked outside the label, bottom dock (zoom, fit, 1:1, Grid, Snap, **Printer view** = exact dots, align/distribute), inspector tabs **Element** (field binding, prefix/suffix, decimals, Indian digit grouping, custom text with `{field}` tokens + Insert field, font family, size mm↔pt, B/I/AA toggles, align/valign, Shrink/Wrap/Cut off, rotation 0/90/180/270, white-on-black, barcode/QR options with live **scan check**, exact X/Y/W/H, centre/full-width), **Label** (12 presets incl. 4 jewellery tail tags and multi-up, W/H, DPI, across/gaps/roll width, shift/darkness/speed/rotate-180, default font, A4-sheet layout with labels-per-page, categories/default/status/code), **Layers** (order, lock, hide); status line (cursor mm, selection, warnings: off-label, unscannable, layout size). Keyboard: arrows 0.1/1 mm, R, Ctrl+D/C/V/Z/Shift+Z/Y/A/S, Del, Esc, +/−/0, G, P. 100-step undo. New-template starter with preset cards. Templates tab is now a card gallery with live thumbnails, Edit/Duplicate and a filter. Codes made from the name and de-duplicated (verified: a second save of the same name became `-2`, not an overwrite).
- [x] **58.3 Any Item field on a label** — `engines.StickerLabel.Fields` (every scalar Item field, ≤500 chars) set in `resolveAndLogSticker`; `StickerFieldText` falls back to it, and now also understands `custom` text with `{field}` tokens and `prefix`/`suffix` (`StickerElement.Kind/Prefix/Suffix`). **Blank-value rule (both renderers)**: a field-bound element with a blank value is left off entirely, and a custom text whose *every* `{field}` is blank prints nothing (no stray "W: gm"). The server-side ZPL path (API clients of the QZ payload endpoint) skips QR/line/box/image kinds instead of misprinting them. Tests: `TestStickerItemFieldsAndFieldFallback`, `TestZPLStickerElementStage58Kinds`, Fields assertion in `TestPrintStickersBarcodeSVG`.
- [x] **58.4 Print flows rewired** — Print tab (manual SKUs and GRN/Transfer document lines) now: `POST /api/v1/stickers/print` (validation, DEVICE-0298 printer check, `sticker_print_log`) → `printStickerRun` → engine: silent TSPL/ZPL through the print agent when the Printer record has a raw language + OS Printer Name and the agent is reachable (job logged to `print_job_log`), else the browser print dialog. **Bug fixed on the way**: a TSPL (TSC) printer used to receive ZPL from `BuildStickerPayload`; the built-in default label is now drawn by the engine too, so it prints on TSC. Old designer code removed from `view-printing.js`/`app.js`; `VIEW_MODULE_VERSION`/`app.js?v` → 34, `styles.css?v` → 27; studio and engine are imported with the view module's own `?v=` so a deploy refreshes them together. No new route, no migration (settings/logos ride in an extra `design` key on the StickerTemplate document; `elements` keeps the Stage-52 shape so old templates open and print unchanged).
- [x] **58.5 Live verification** (scratch server :8147 against the local DB, Playwright + Chrome). Found and fixed during it: (a) the studio's `data-act` buttons collided with the app-wide `[data-act]` dispatcher ("no such action function: drawer") — renamed `data-sa`; (b) the environment banner covered the studio's top bar — studio now starts below it; (c) the grid auto-placed the stage into the closed drawer's column — explicit grid columns; (d) a drag never moved because selecting re-renders the overlay node holding pointer capture — tracking moved to `window`; (e) shortcuts were swallowed because the name box kept focus and the dialog guard matched the app's always-present closed `.modal-overlay` nodes — canvas clicks release field focus, guard checks `.modal-overlay.open`/`#custom-dialog-container:not(.hidden)`; (f) clicking one element of a multi-selection could not narrow it; (g) rotating a long text sideways on a 12 mm tag pushed it off the label — now kept inside; (h) gallery thumbnails showed "W: gm" — blank-value rule + sample values. Final runs: drag with snap guide, undo/redo exact, Shift+→ = 1 mm, marquee 5/5, align tools, resize, save/reopen, 92×12 at 300 dpi = 1087×142-dot TSPL/ZPL with `PRINT 1,3`/`^PQ3`, browser sheet `@page` sizes per template, Print tab manual run → sheet (2 pages for 2 copies) and → stubbed agent (TSPL base64 to `TSC TE310`, `Submitted/TSPL` logged, history rows written); light + dark themes screenshotted; zero page errors. Fixtures (4 templates, 1 printer, 3 sticker-log + 1 job-log rows, showcase template) deleted afterwards. `go build ./...`, `go vet ./engines/`, `go test ./engines/ -run 'Sticker|ZPL|QZ'` green.
- [x] **58.6 Guides** — USER_GUIDE §7A.2 rewritten as a full studio walkthrough (+3 "How do I…" rows, intro note on TSPL/ZPL Printer fields, glossary); ADMIN_GUIDE §B.3.4 updated (rendering, DPI precedence, Item fields, API-client ZPL path, storage limits, test prints not logged; the "byte-identical with no template" claim corrected); QZ_PRINTING_SETUP.md template section replaced. `go run ./cmd/doclint -root .` — no new findings (KB generated-drift warnings pre-date this stage).
- [ ] **58.7 Physical printer check** — not possible on this machine (no TSC/Zebra attached; same gap as 31.1.8). On the first real run confirm: TSPL bitmap polarity (TSPL prints a 0 bit; the engine inverts) and `DIRECTION 1` orientation — if a tag comes out upside down, tick **Rotate the print 180°** on the template; check a 203-dpi Code 128 scans at the chosen width. **[needs user: one test print on the shop's TSC/Zebra]**
  — *2026-10-18: still open. The studio is live on production since 2026-10-09 (58.8), but no print on a physical TSC or Zebra has been recorded yet, here or under 31.1.8 (the same hardware gap). Nothing can be closed from this machine; it closes on the user's first real test print — a correctly oriented, scannable tag closes it; an inverted (white-on-black) TSC tag means the TSPL bitmap polarity must be flipped in `buildTSPL`.*
- [x] **58.8 Release** — done: committed (`068a665`, `f6e3961`), pushed and deployed to production on 2026-10-09 at the user's go-ahead; details below.
  — *2026-10-09: released with the five-item pass (51.10, 52.9, 57.15, 57.8) at the user's go-ahead.* Commits `068a665` (code, staged hunk-by-hunk around the Stage 55/56/59 sessions' uncommitted work; verified as a tree in a detached LF worktree: build, vet, full `go test ./... -p 1`) and `f6e3961` (gendocs/genkb/brainmap/surfacescan regenerated in that clean worktree, so generated docs describe committed code only), pushed (origin moved from `9f3018a`, so `2065ff4`/`53c7d10` went up too). **KB drift:** `genkb -check` was already clean - the unrelated drift had been fixed elsewhere - so the step was gendocs then genkb. **Release-gate fixes found by the full suite:** Stage 58's `.stk-*` CSS (appended to `styles.css`) pushed the cold-core budget over; moved unchanged to `public/sticker-studio.css`, linked on demand by `sticker-engine.js` (`stickerStylesReady`) - committed LF cold core 183,588/184,320 (previous release 183,728); and PurchaseReturn's scope entry moved to the location-optional block. Note the budget test reads files from disk, so a Windows CRLF checkout over-reports (~185k) - measure on LF bytes. Prod: encrypted backup `custom_erp_20261009T002850Z.dump.enc` (sha256 OK) + rollback snapshot `/opt/erp/rollback_20261009T002850Z`; deploy from the worktree's `deploy.ps1` -> DEPLOY-OK, 4 migrations applied (52_9, 57_offer_group_targeting - from the earlier undeployed `2065ff4` - 57_8, 57_15), none touching users or passwords; both tenant schemas (`tenant_default`, `tenant_minn`) carry the new column, fields and PRT/DN/AST series. https://app.wholeops.in/ 200, `/api/v1/health` ok, `/api/v1/version` `f6e3961`. ## Stage 60 — Number series per transaction and store, item types with barcode rules and behaviour, names not codes, platform data console (opened 2026-10-14 from the user's "Build")

**Why it was opened.** The user asked whether PO/GRN/barcode were ready and found: numbers carry the store *code* and a year (`GRN/HQ/26-27/000001`), there is no suffix and no per-store prefix, the barcode rule is one switch per client, `Item.item_type` is only Stock/Fixed Asset, codes still show in some screens, and there is no way for a platform super user to fix data inside a client.

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-14):**
- Lengths: purchase series (PR, RFQ, QTN, PO, ASN, GRN, PRT, DN) **5 digits**; stock/sales/production/asset (TO, TI, SI, PRO, AST) **6**; HR (LV, ATT, EXP, LOAN, GRV, Payslip) **5**. Each transaction keeps its own row - formats differ per transaction.
- The store segment inside a number is the store's **Short Code** (falls back to Store Code when blank). A store carries Store Name, Short Code, Store Code and **Facility Code**; Facility Code is the one OMS uses.
- Item types: **barcode rule + behaviour** (Gift / Free-with-item sell at zero price, Backflush is consumed at production completion).
- Platform console: **edit every table, raw**, in any client, customer personal fields masked, every change audited.
- "No code in UI" means internal record codes. Shown as codes on purpose: document numbers (PO/GRN...), Design ID, Combination ID, barcode. Everything else shows its name (vendor, store, product...).

- [x] **60.1 Number series.** Built and verified 2026-10-14. `db/migrations_stage60_1_number_series.sql`: `prefix_configs.suffix` + `store_overrides` (JSONB, store code → prefix/suffix); AST/Payslip/PRT rows in the engine's existing fallback shape; lengths per the user's answer (purchase + HR 5, rest 6), applied only to rows still at the shipped 6. New `engines/number_series.go` (`ListNumberSeries`/`SaveNumberSeries`, validation `ADMINC-0311`, cross-series prefix collision `ADMINC-0310` covering store overrides, `storeNumberSegment` = Short Code unless shared). `GenerateSequence` reads suffix + override; counter stays keyed by Store Code so a short-code change never restarts a series. `handlePrefix` delegates to the engine. Screen: one form (lazy-built in `view-admin.js`, not in `index.html` - cold-core budget) with live preview and a store-wise prefix/suffix table listing every Location. Test `TestNumberSeriesStoreSegmentSuffixAndOverrides`; browser: edit/save/restore PO, GRN→"PO" refused with the catalog message.
- [x] **60.2 Store identity.** `db/migrations_stage60_2_store_identity.sql`: `Location.facility_code`; labels Store Code / Store Name / Short Code (printed in document numbers) / Facility Code (used by OMS), only where still at shipped text. `validateLocationIdentityCodes`: Short Code unique against short codes and store codes (`MASTER-0312`), Facility Code unique (`MASTER-0313`). `ResolveFacilityCode` in `ImportChannelSalesOrder` maps an OMS facility to its store (store codes still pass through). *Not done:* outbound inventory sync to Unicommerce/Magento still sends Store Code - no connector reads a facility today.
- [x] **60.3 Item Type master + barcode rules.** `db/migrations_stage60_3_item_types.sql`: `ItemType` master (7 seeded: Product default, Accessory, Non-Asset, Free With Item, Gift, Backflush, Fixed Asset); `Item.item_type` Select → Link (only the shipped shape converted; stored values untouched, blank/"Stock" read as Product and normalised in `validateDocumentWith` on next save); `Item.barcode_generate_at`/`barcode_per_receipt_date`/`free_with`. `engines/item_types.go`: precedence Item > Item Type > client setting; per-tenant cache (30 s, dropped on ItemType save). `PrepareItemBarcode` and `EnsureReceiptBarcodes` now per item. Provisioning copies the ItemType rows to new tenants (`saas.go`). `IsFixedAssetItem(tenantID, data)` reads the type's flag.
- [x] **60.4 Item type behaviour.** POS quote: zero-price types price at 0 with source `item_type_free` (reference 0 too, so not a discount; overrides cannot apply); Free With Item needs `free_with` in the same cart at ≥ qty (`POSOFF-0314`), Item save needs a parent that is not itself (`MASTER-0315`). Manufacturing: Backflush components skipped at Material Issue (`splitBackflushComponents`, recorded as `backflush_components`) and consumed per completed qty in the same ledger posting as the finished goods (`backflushLines`, `MANUFA-0143` on shortage). Test `TestItemTypesBarcodePricingAndBackflush` (precedence, free/gift quote, parent rule, backflush issue/partial completion stock).
- [x] **60.5 Names, not codes.** One choke point: `sweepLinkNames` now also infers a column's record type from its header (Vendor/Supplier, Store/Location/Warehouse/From/To, Customer, SKU/Item/Product, with optional "Code"/"(…)" tails) via `COLUMN_LINK_RE`, so every table built anywhere shows names; Design/Combination/Barcode headers deliberately excluded; `linkNameMap.complete` skips per-cell lookups when all records fitted. Receipts/invoices: `ReceiptStore.Line()` prints the name only (code only when no name), invoice payload names customer + store. Labels: VendorInvoice "Vendor", PO "Vendor (auto)" (`migrations_stage60_5_name_labels.sql`). Browser: PO list Vendor column shows "Nike Corp". *Left:* Data fields that hold codes by hand (Batch/Serial "Item Code (SKU)") are still typed as codes in their forms (lists show names); BOM picker (57.26 e).
- [x] **60.6 Platform data console.** `engines/platform_console.go` + `internal/server/handlers_platform_console.go`, routes `/api/v1/platform/*` (LevelAdmin + `ERP_PLATFORM_OPERATORS` allowlist of `client:username`, `PLATFORM-0317`), unknown client ids refused (GetTenantSchema would fall back to default). Every table of any client, paged, key-column search only; passwords/secrets/credentials and customer PII masked in columns and inside JSON (restored on save; a moved mask is refused); keys, masked columns, audit tables and key-less tables not editable (`PLATFORM-0316`); values cast by Postgres; before/after (masked) to the client's and the operator's audit log as `PLATFORM_DATA_EDIT`. Screen Admin → Data Console. Test `TestPlatformConsoleMaskingAndEdits`; browser: masked customers and users, an edit with confirm, Store Manager 403. **Finding (not changed, needs decision):** the older cross-tenant endpoints (`GET /api/v1/admin/tenants`, tenant module entitlements, tenant usage) gate on `IsSuperAdmin` only, and every client has its own Super Admin - so a client's admin can list all clients and change another client's entitlements. Decided 2026-10-15 (user: "Only Super admin I can, no one else. Client can't change other") - see 60.8.
- [x] **60.7 Guides + docs.** ADMIN_GUIDE B.3.0.3a (store identities), B.3.2 rewritten (form, suffix, store-wise, lengths, enforced prefix rule), barcode policy per type/item, Item Types section, C.7 Data Console; USER_GUIDE §6.1 example, §6.8 item types + index row; `deploy/erp.env.example` documents `ERP_PLATFORM_OPERATORS`; brain map claims the 4 new files; attack surface regenerated.
- [x] **60.8 Cross-client lock-down (user, 2026-10-15: "Only Super admin I can, no one else. Client can't change other").** Every endpoint that reaches another client now requires a platform operator (`requirePlatformOperator`, `PLATFORM-0317`): list clients, tenant usage, set module entitlement, set package, set hostname, provision client, sandbox create/list/reset/delete. `allowTenantTarget`: a client's own Super Admin may still read its own module entitlements and version record, never another client's. Unchanged on purpose: the global module/package catalogs and the per-client feature flag (acts only on the caller's own client). `package_selection_stage50_test.go` registers its admin as operator; new `TestCrossClientEndpointsNeedPlatformOperator`. *Deploy note:* the operator's own login must be in `ERP_PLATFORM_OPERATORS` before release, or nobody can reach Tenant Entitlements/Usage. Verified 2026-10-15: full `go test -p 1 ./...` green; over HTTP a non-operator Super Admin gets 403 on `/admin/tenants`, the operator 200.
- [x] **60.9 One table per transaction in the Data Console (user, 2026-10-15: "I need individual table for all txn ... I should be fixing it").** Storage stays the one `documents` table (a physical split would move every client's live data and every module's queries - against the additive-schema rule and the client-data rule); the console presents each record type as its own table instead: `GET /api/v1/platform/tenants/{t}/records` (record types with counts), `.../records/{doctype}` (one column per `doctype_fields` field + id/status/timestamps + `other_data` for undefined keys; search by number/code/name, never hidden values), `POST .../records/{doctype}/{id}` (typed write-back per field type - Number, Check, JSONTable/JSONMap; status kept in step in column and data; hidden fields refused; JSON keeps hidden keys). Screen: table list grouped by module, Select fields as dropdowns, Link fields with the app's record picker. Test `TestPlatformRecordTablesReadAndEdit`. ADMIN_GUIDE C.7 rewritten. Verified 2026-10-15 in the browser: 17 module groups + 67 database tables listed, PurchaseOrder shows 14 rows with labelled columns, a status edit saved and was put back. Check fields edit as true/false.
- **Verification (2026-10-14):** `go build`/`go vet` clean; full `go test -p 1 ./...` green (engines, internal/server, db, cmd/*, securityscan, docgen, kb, supplychain); release budget cold core 184,099 / 184,320 after moving the series form and `prefixConfigSample` out of the startup files and condensing two comments. Found by the suite and fixed: the item-type normalisation made every import row miss the batch Link prefetch (64 s for 20,000 rows) - `validateDocumentWith` now remembers Link hits for the rest of the file (2.2 s). **Not deployed; not committed.** Deploy needs the new binary and all four 60.x migrations together (an old binary against the migrated schema rejects "Stock" item types), and `ERP_PLATFORM_OPERATORS` set on the server.

## Stage 61 — GRN batch numbers and scan-to-age lookup (opened + built 2026-10-15 from the user's request)

**Why it was opened.** The user: *"Introduce a batch number as well. Same barcode if we are generating, it should have a batch number in GRN"* — then *"in each GRN's each Combination ID/SKU will have a batch number"*, for **every SKU** — and *"I should be able to know batch code easily after scanning, so I will know ageing or which day it came in"*. A SKU's barcode stays the same on every receipt (Stage 57.7 default), so nothing on a tag said which delivery it came from.

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-15):** one batch number per **GRN × SKU**; applies to **every SKU**. Taken without asking (reversible, flag if wrong): the number carries its receipt date (`B261015-0001`) so the day is readable off the tag; a **batch-tracked** item still needs the carton's lot typed (INVENT-0115 is a per-item control the client chose; an invented number would hide a missed entry); Fixed Asset items are not numbered (never stock).

- [x] **61.1 Batch number on every GRN line.** `engines.PrepareGRNBatchNumbers` (`engines/wms_receiving.go`), called from the GRN create path in `handlers_core_doc_engine.go` next to `PrepareGRNReceipt`, before the document is written — so the GRN, its stickers, the ledger and the Batch record all carry one value. Blank lines only; a typed number is kept and covers that SKU's other blank lines; edits are never renumbered. Counter: `GRNBatchSeq` (daily bucket); format fixed in code (`grnBatchFromParts`), counter read as the digit run after the day segment, so it is right with or without the series row. `db/migrations_stage61_1_grn_batch.sql`: `GRNBatchSeq` series row + `Batch.grn` / `Batch.received_on` fields on every tenant (additive, ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING). `EnsureBatch` stamps `grn` + `received_on` on a lot a GRN registers. GRN form: placeholder "auto if blank" and a note giving the format for a non-tracked SKU.
- [x] **61.2 Scan to see receipt date and age.** Report `batch-ageing` — **Batch Lookup & Ageing** (Report Catalog → Inventory, not WMS-gated): scan a batch number (case-insensitive), a SKU's barcode/receipt-date barcode/code (`ResolveItemBySKU`), or leave blank; oldest first; **Older than (days)** filter. Columns: Batch, Item, Received On, Age (days), GRN, Supplier, Received Qty, Expires, Batch Status. Shows what each batch *received*, not what is left — a POS sale scans the SKU barcode and records no batch (said in both guides). Lots from before 61.1 age from their Batch record's creation date. Report Catalog: **Enter in any filter runs the report** (one delegated listener on `#rc-params`) so a scanner works without clicking Run.
- [x] **61.3 Batch on the sticker.** The built-in default label (`DEFAULT_STICKER_TEMPLATE` in `public/sticker-engine.js`) gains a last line `Batch: {batch_no}` (barcode 13 → 10.4 mm high to make room); the blank-value rule leaves it off for a label with no batch. Custom templates add the existing **Batch / Lot** field in the Studio (text, or a Barcode element bound to it). `VIEW_MODULE_VERSION`/`app.js?v` 36 → 37.

## Stage 62 — Fast-fashion buying: designs and combinations inside the PO, multi-PO GRN at invoice price, automatic debit/credit notes, standard prints (opened 2026-10-15 from the user's "Build everything not built yet … don't ask, start building")

**Why it was opened.** The user's buying flow: *"In PO it will ask vendor first, then item will be created and SKU can be created in PO itself, or type design and load all combinations and we can remove a few"*; *"while creating PO it should ask all matrix of product defined in the org; once all filled it will create design and variant"*; a **design number** that is either *always new* or *reused when every attribute matches*, per config, and a pasted existing design number that *always takes the same design* while allowing new combinations; *"GRN from multiple PO of the same vendor"*; *"GRN we will have credit note and debit note but never be hold"*; *"PO price and received invoice price — two columns; vendor's invoice amount at the top to type; it will generate the diff and the credit note and debit note; a section to see"*; *"std print profile for PO, GRN, credit note, debit note"*. IRN later (not in this stage).

**Decisions taken without asking (user: "everything recommended approved, don't ask") — reversible, flag if wrong:**
- **Design = `ProductFamily`** (already numbered `Design/...` since 51.5); **combination = `Item`** with `family`/`parent_product_code` = the design and `variant_option_values` = its attribute values. No new design master.
- **"All matrix defined in the org" = every Active `ProductAttributeDef` of Value Type Select** (its `value_options`). Pick one value → a design-level attribute; pick several → an axis; the combinations are the cross product of the axes, any of which can be removed before creating.
- Items are created **through the ordinary record API** (`POST /api/v1/doc/ProductFamily`, `/doc/Item`), so numbering, variant code, barcode policy, duplicate-combination checks and audit all apply unchanged.
- **GRN prices are ex-GST per unit.** PO Rate is read from the PO by the server (never trusted from the screen); Invoice Rate defaults to it and is editable.
- **Stock and GRN Suspense (2100) are booked at the Invoice Rate** — the vendor's bill then matches its GRN exactly, which is what removes the hold.
- **The PO price is the agreed price.** Invoice Rate above PO Rate → a **Debit Note** to the vendor for the excess; below → a **Vendor Credit Note** for the shortfall; both incl. GST at the line's rate, **posted immediately** (never Draft, never held). GL: Debit Note Dr 2100 / Cr 5150 (taxable) / Cr 1500 (GST); Vendor Credit Note the mirror. Net payable and net cost both land on the PO value.
- The **vendor invoice amount typed at the top** is a control total: the screen shows lines total vs typed total and the difference, and the GRN records it, but it does not block or hold.
- **Multi-PO GRN:** every line carries its own `po_id`; the header keeps `po_id` (first PO, unchanged for every existing reader) plus `po_ids`. All POs must be the same vendor. Every PO check (approval, over-receipt, unexpected item, fully received) runs per PO on that PO's lines.

- [x] **62.1 Multi-PO GRN (same vendor).** `engines/grn_pricing.go` `PrepareGRNPricing` (create only, first in the GRN create path of `handlers_core_doc_engine.go`) resolves each line's `po_id` (blank → header PO), sets header `po_id` (first PO, so every existing reader keeps working) + `po_ids`, fills `vendor`, refuses mixed vendors (**GOODSR-0318**, `error_catalog_extensions.go`). `validateGRNRules` loops `GRNPOIDs` and runs the existing approval / PURCHA-0084/0087/0088 checks per PO on that PO's lines (`validateGRNAgainstPO`); `fetchGRNReceivedQuantities` finds GRNs by `po_id` or `po_ids` and counts only that PO's lines (`grnReceivedLine.POID`). Screen: **Add PO & Load Items** adds (chips; × removes a PO with its lines), refuses another vendor, loads only **open** qty (earlier non-cancelled GRNs subtracted), PO column when >1 PO; list shows every PO, newest first.
- [x] **62.2 PO Rate + Invoice Rate on every GRN line; vendor invoice no / date / amount.** Server stamps `po_rate`/`gst_rate` from each line's PO via `PreviewPurchaseOrder` (a client value is ignored — verified with `po_rate: 1`); `invoice_rate` defaults to it (negative → **GOODSR-0319**); header `po_value`, `invoice_value`, `invoice_gst`, `invoice_total`, `invoice_amount_difference`. Costing: `RecordGRNReceiptCosting` and the landed-cost voucher use `grnLineUnitCostsPaise` (accepted-qty blend of invoice rates); PO rate only for pre-62 GRNs. Screen: editable Invoice Rate (row highlighted, ± per unit vs PO), invoice header row, totals panel — the "section to see": PO value, invoice value, GST, incl. GST, typed amount, invoice minus lines, which notes will be raised.
- [x] **62.3 Automatic Debit Note / Vendor Credit Note, never held.** `PostGRNRateDifferenceNotes` after the stock post: above PO → `DebitNote` (series DN) via `PostDebitNote` + its GST leg Dr 2100 / Cr 1500; below → new `VendorCreditNote` (series VCN) via `PostVendorCreditNote` (Dr 5150 / Cr 2100 + Dr 1500 / Cr 2100). Idempotent (one of each per GRN; GST leg keyed); a post failure comes back as `notes_warning`, the receipt stands. GRN linked (`debit_note_id` / `vendor_credit_note_id`) — **bug found by the test and fixed**: the link update cast jsonb to text and silently failed. Vendor invoice: `readPricedGRNBilling` values a priced GRN at invoice rate + line GST across all its POs and skips the PO over-billing check (`grnBilling.Priced`), so the bill **Matches** instead of MismatchHold. Notes screen: Vendor Credit Notes panel, GRN / GST / Total columns, Print on every note. Migration `db/migrations_stage62_1_po_design_grn_pricing.sql` (VendorCreditNote doctype / fields / perms; DebitNote, GRN, ProductFamily fields; VCN + DN series). Payables role template operates VendorCreditNote.
- [x] **62.4 Standard prints** — delivered as Stage 63's built-in Standard layout per transaction (no hand-coded sheets). The old PO print sheet and its `styles.css` rules were retired, freeing cold-core bytes.
- [x] **62.5 Design & combinations inside the PO.** "+ Add design" (vendor first): every Active ProductAttributeDef (`GET /api/v1/procurement/design-matrix`); Select values as chips (one = design attribute, several = axis; All / None), Data / Number typed; combinations grid (qty / price / MRP per row, × remove, restore); **Create & add** creates the ProductFamily and one Item per combination through the ordinary record API and adds PO lines. A design created with no code is numbered server-side (`PrepareDesignNumber`, `Design/...` series; a typed code is kept). **Pre-existing data-loss bug fixed:** creating an Item whose generated Combination ID already existed silently overwrote that Item (upsert on the shared id) — `PrepareItemVariantCode` now refuses it.
- [x] **62.6 Design number mode** `procurement.po_design_number_mode` = new (default) / match (`FindMatchingDesign`: same name, case / space-insensitive, + identical normalised design attributes). A pasted design no → `GET /api/v1/procurement/design/{code}` loads its attributes and existing combinations (shown with SKU, reused); new values add combinations.
- [x] **62.7 Guides, tests, live check.** USER_GUIDE §6 steps 3/7/9, §6.6, new §6.9 and §6.10, §7 step 7, seven "How do I" rows; ADMIN_GUIDE §B.3.6. Tests `TestStage62GRNPricingAndNotes` (9 subtests), `TestStage62Designs`. Live (scratch :8162, local DB :5490, both migrations applied): API run — design with 4 combos, duplicate refused, match / no-match, 3 approved POs, mixed vendor refused, multi-PO GRN → DN 360 + 18 and VCN 60 + 3 posted, GL checked, over-receipt per PO, vendor invoice Matched at 8,883 against a 6,300 PO. Chrome (Playwright): Add design 4 → 3 combos onto the PO, pasted design shows its SKUs; GRN with two POs, rates edited, totals, Post → toast with both notes, row shows invoice + notes. Found in the browser and fixed: dark-theme table styles leaked into the printed page.

## Stage 63 — Print Template Studio for transactions (opened 2026-10-15 from the user's message during Stage 62)

**Why it was opened.** The user, mid-Stage 62: *"Print template should be customisable by the user. Drag and drop from master table. Placement for each template. Similar to barcode print, drag and drop from product attribute. I can map product-wise different templates. Each transaction should have its template: PO one page, Transfer Out one page, Transfer In, GRN, Vendor PO template — Vendor PO may have images, in Excel, or exact product detail. My brand name will always be there. There should be a selection of which store I am generating from, and a place in the template for it. Store address, state, product detail, tax bifurcation, total and everything should be there."*

**Decisions taken without asking (user: "don't ask, start building") — reversible, flag if wrong:**
- **One document designer, not a second sticker studio.** A transaction print is a page with flowing line tables and a tax summary, so it renders to HTML pages (exact on any printer, PDF via the browser); the Sticker Studio stays the thermal-label designer. Same way of working: drag fields from a searchable field list, inspector, mm positions, snap.
- **Print data comes from one server endpoint per document** (header fields, brand, generating store, vendor / other store, every line with its Item master fields and image, GST split per rate, totals, amount in words), so a template can place *any* field and every transaction prints the same way.
- **Templates are `PrintTemplate` records** per transaction (PO, Vendor PO, GRN, Transfer Out, Transfer In, Debit Note, Vendor Credit Note), with a default per transaction and optional **mapping by vendor and by product family/category** — the most specific match wins, and the user can still pick another at print time.
- **Brand always prints:** setting `print.brand_name` (blank = the legal entity's name); a template without a brand element gets it in the page header automatically.
- **"Which store I am generating from"** is chosen at print time (defaults to the document's own store); its name, address, state, GSTIN fill the store block.
- **Vendor PO to Excel** is a hand-written .xlsx (standard library zip/XML, no new dependency) with the item image in a column when the item has one.

- [x] **63.1 Print data endpoint** `GET /api/v1/print/document/{txn}/{id}?store=&images=1` (`engines/print_documents.go` `BuildPrintDocument`): PurchaseOrder, VendorPO, GRN, TransferOut, TransferIn, DebitNote, VendorCreditNote → brand, generating store (Location + its Legal Entity's state / GSTIN), party, ship-to, header fields, lines with `item.*` and an optional embedded thumbnail, GST split per rate (intra / inter), totals with round-off, amount in words, plus the matching templates. Catalogue `GET /api/v1/print/catalog?txn=` (doctype fields incl. custom ones, blocks, totals, line + every Item field). Transfers: value at `standard_cost` / MRP; same GSTIN = delivery challan (no GST), different GSTIN = taxable (IGST across states).
- [x] **63.2 `PrintTemplate` doctype + mapping** (`db/migrations_stage63_1_print_templates.sql`, layout max_length 1.5 MB; `engines/print_templates.go`): vendor 8 / family 4 / category 2 / default 1; a mapping that does not fit excludes the template; built-in Standard layout per transaction in `trade-docs.js`. Setting `print.brand_name`.
- [x] **63.3 Template renderer** (`public/trade-docs.js` + `public/trade-docs.css`, loaded on demand — cold core untouched): text with `{tokens|format}`, field, store / party / ship-to blocks, image / logo, line, box, items table flowing across pages (rows measured, header repeated, continuation start Y), tax summary, totals, page no; first / every / last page; brand auto-added when not placed; blank info pieces drop out.
- [x] **63.4 Print Template Studio** (`public/print-template-studio.js`): searchable field list, drag onto the page (header fields) or onto the table (columns), Insert palette, drag / resize with snap and guides, inspector (position, pages, font / bold / italic / align / colour / background / border; per type: tokens, field + format, block lines, logo upload ≤400 KB, table columns editor, totals rows), undo / redo, keyboard, Preview with a real document, Test print, template settings + mapping, Save. Gallery = Stock → Sticker Printing → **Document templates** tab. Starts below the environment banner (found live: the banner covered Save).
- [x] **63.5 Print anywhere** — print window (Print as, template, generating store, preview, Print, Excel, Design templates) on: PO rows (QZ silent print first when a PO printer is mapped), GRN rows, the GRN Price Notes column, the Debit / Credit Notes screen, Transfer rows (Print Transfer Out from Approved, Print Transfer In once Received).
- [x] **63.6 Vendor PO Excel** — `engines/xlsx_writer.go` (stdlib zip / XML, one-cell-anchored pictures) + `GET /api/v1/procurement/purchase-order/{id}/excel`.
- [x] **63.7 Guides, tests, live check.** USER_GUIDE §7B (+ §7A intro); ADMIN_GUIDE §B.3.7. Tests `TestPrintTemplateMapping`, `TestWriteXLSX`, and the print subtest of the Stage 62 test. Live: prints for all 5 document types via the API (brand, store state / GSTIN, tax split, words), catalogue 68 header / 53 line fields, Excel download; browser: GRN print preview, studio drag field + drag column + Shift+↓ / undo + save, the saved template offered as "best match" with its Barcode column printed, switch back to Standard, PO → Vendor PO with the Image column, Excel download. **Not checked:** a real item picture in Excel / print (fixtures had no PIM media; the drawing part is unit-tested), a physical printer.

## Stage 64 — Every client kept in step with tenant_default; every data issue in the client's log (opened + built 2026-10-16 while preparing the minn.wholeops.in handover)

**Why it was opened.** The user: *"test the PO and print in app.wholeops.in, close and hand over to minn.wholeops.in client. I need all basic features working"*, then *"all logs should be captured if a data issue is found, I should be able to browse the db and fix it in both instances"* and *"don't do partial partial"*. A schema comparison on production found `tenant_minn` (provisioned 2026-09-22) missing **16 tables** (`print_job_log`, `bin_allocation`, `bin_stock_batch`, `bin_stock_owner`, `warehouse_owner`, `command_idempotency`, `mfa_recovery_codes`, the 4 audit archive tables, Pine Labs + Unicommerce), **11 columns** (`audit_logs.seq/signature/sig_version/entity_*/correlation_id` — every audit write on minn failed, its last audit row is 2026-09-22 — plus `users.credential_version`, `inventory_availability.hold_qty`, `sticker_print_log.source_*/template_id`), **53 record types** (Batch, StickerTemplate, UOM, SerialNumber, Zone, PhysicalInventory, PasswordResetRequest…) with **446 fields**, their permissions, **12 number series**, all **230 StatusTransitionRule** records, the **INR** Currency and the 4 TDS sections. 10 of its tables drew ids from `tenant_default`'s sequences. GL accounts, settings, entitlements, feature flags, field permissions matched.

**Decisions taken without asking (reversible, flag if wrong):** insert-only — nothing a client has is changed or removed; default permissions only for record types the sync itself adds (a permission an admin removed is not handed back); system seed records = `StatusTransitionRule`, `Currency`, `TDSSection` only; never copied: business records, users, settings, entitlements, approval rules, `sequence_counters`.

- [x] **64.1 Client schema sync at both choke points.** `db/tenant_schema_sync.go` `SyncTenantSchemaTx` / `SyncAllTenantSchemas`, from the catalog (no hand-kept list): missing tables (`LIKE … INCLUDING ALL`), columns (type, default, NOT NULL; a nextval default gets the client's own sequence), indexes (compared by definition, so a LIKE-renamed index is not duplicated), own sequences for serial columns drawing from another schema (started after the column's max), record types / fields / number series, default permissions for added record types, system seed records; least-privilege grants when `erp_app` exists; each statement under a savepoint, a failure is a `[sync-warn]` line. Runs at the end of every `erp-server -migrate` (`cmd/server/main.go`, one `[sync]` line per changed client) and inside `ProvisionTenantSchema` (`engines/saas.go`; the grant helper moved to `db.GrantLeastPrivilegeSchemaAccessTx`). Test `TestSyncTenantSchemaBringsStaleClientUpToTemplate` (stale client → no table / column / record type / field / rule missing, no foreign sequence, own sequence after existing ids, an existing record type's permissions not handed back, audit insert with `signature` works, second run changes nothing).
- [x] **64.2 Background failures reach the client's error log.** `engines/logs.go` `LogBackgroundJobError(module, what, schema, err)`: server log every time (same text as before), the client's `system_error_logs` once an hour per distinct failure. 11 per-client sites: Pine Labs reconciliation, job runner claim + retention sweep, report export query + sweep, scheduled reports, dashboard digest, PIM export / import schedules, wave auto-create, environment quarantine sweep. Test `TestLogBackgroundJobErrorRecordsOncePerHour`.
- [x] **64.3 Guides.** ADMIN_GUIDE §C.6.1 (what the sync adds / never touches, the deploy output, `[sync-warn]`); §C.7 "Finding the log for a data issue" (`system_error_logs` / `audit_logs` per client in the Data Console).
- [x] **64.4 Production (2026-10-16, `042579b`).** Before: backup `custom_erp_20261016T030115Z.dump.enc` (sha256 OK), rollback `/opt/erp/rollback_20261016_030115`, `erp.env` copied to `erp.env.bak_20261016_030115`; `ERP_PLATFORM_OPERATORS=default:admin` added (user's choice). ## Stage 65 — PO: pinned create zone, role-controlled delete, item name → design → variants, operator control panel (opened 2026-10-16 from the user's messages during the minn handover)

**Why it was opened.** The user: *"PO headers will be frozen, all totals everything in top frozen. If I add a new item it will get added and scrolled down, but my screen will be the create zone only always. Old added items I scroll down and access. I should be able to delete it as well. Once a PO is created I should be able to delete it as well. It will be user/role controlled — give it in role level config"*; *"Add design … it should always ask item name, all attributes. Two parts while creating a PO, parent and child. Parent = item name, HSN, tax. Child = variant attributes, several per parent. Once added it auto-creates the design id and combination id. HSN, once defined for an item name, never asked again; item name shown in a dropdown. Item name made of two parts: Category and Product Type. I can add several design numbers, typing a design number suggests as I type, and if not found it won't reorder / repopulate"*; and *"a page where I can see all clients and what access I gave, release and all other control, where hosted and everything"*.

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-16, asked):** PO delete = **any PO with no goods received** (any status), for roles with Delete on Purchase Order. Item name = **new Category + Product Type masters**, item name holds HSN + GST once. Variant attributes = **PIM Product Attributes, new values / attributes addable inline from the PO**. Design number = **under the item name** (item name → designs → variant rows → combination ids), typed with as-you-type suggestions.

## Stage 66 — Smart HSN and GST rate suggestions; no number spinners anywhere (opened + built 2026-10-17 from the user's New Item screenshot)

User, 2026-10-17, on the empty HSN box of New Item: *"Need to suggest HSN for the Industry. Need to make smart. Once Industry selected load those hsn. If silver not silver from item name, Cloth, fabric.. All aspect it should be smart to suggest. User will select the correct one from those recommendations or if not suggest, Type and Add. But we will have a master hsn list present"*, then *"Same goes for gst rate"*, *"hsn rate will be correlated"*, and *"No number updown in numbers anywhere. It is a thumbrule for all UI. But number section will accept number that is correct."*

- [x] **66.1 HSN master list + ranked suggestions.** `engines/hsn_master.json` (embedded via `go:embed`): 290 HSN/SAC codes across all ten industry profiles, each with a description, its GST rate(s) (GST 2.0, in force from 22 Sep 2025; price-banded codes carry both rates with notes, e.g. garments/made-ups/footwear 5% up to Rs 2,500 a piece, 18% above), the industries it belongs to, **keywords** (decide *which* code: the material or the exact product, score 3), **related** words (what *kind* of thing, score 1) and **exclusions** ("german silver", "gold plated" are not precious metal). Hindi retail words included (haar, chandi, kangan, jhumka, dahi, sabzi…); spellings folded (aluminum, jewelry, t-shirt, tyre/tire). Rates checked against the 56th GST Council summary; codes whose rate could not be confirmed (2201 water, 7103 loose stones, clay bricks, concrete blocks, organic manure, wooden joinery) ship **without** a rate rather than a guessed one. `engines/hsn_suggest.go` `SuggestHSN` ranks: the client's own Items/Item Names sharing words with the name (4 per shared word, "used on your item …"), the master list (+1 in the client's industry), HSN-list description matches; agreement between sources adds one; weak matches under a third of the best are dropped. With no name: the client's HSN list, then **Common in <industry>**. Typing searches everything (digits by code prefix, words by description/keywords). `GET /api/v1/hsn/suggest?name=&q=&hsn=` (`internal/server/handlers_hsn.go`; gated on HSNCode read, item names quoted only for Item / ItemName the role can read; route classified `doc.default`; attack surface 521 routes). `EnsureHSNCode` now stores the master description for a code learned from a saved Item.
- [x] **66.2 Choosing the industry loads its HSN codes.** `SwitchIndustryProfile` seeds the industry's master codes into the client's HSN Code list inside its transaction (`seedIndustryHSNCodes`; `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, so a description/rate the client set is never touched; single-rate codes get it as Default GST Rate, price-banded ones none). Clients whose industry was set before Stage 66 are not back-filled: their HSN box still shows **Common in <industry>** from the master list, only the HSN Code list page lacks those rows until they are used or the industry is overridden.
- [x] **66.3 GST rate follows the HSN.** Picking a code fills the rate when the code has one rate and the box is empty or was filled by the picker; a price-banded code clears a picker-filled rate so the user chooses; a typed/clicked rate is never overwritten. Under the GST box: the code's own rates as buttons (with when each applies), then 0 / 3 / 5 / 18 / 40. A client's HSN-list rate wins and is listed first as *Set on your HSN list* (so a stale pre-GST-2.0 12% is visible, not hidden). Picker lives in `public/view-pickers.js` (`attachSmartHSNPicker`, CSS injected with the module); `app.js` `attachHSNPicker` is a stub that loads it, plus a generic `opts.source` / `opts.titleFn` on `attachTypeahead`. Applies to every form with an HSN box (Item, Item Name, design), full form and quick-create.
- [x] **66.4 0% needs a Tax Treatment, asked in quick-create.** MASTER-0044 rejects a 0% Item without Exempt / Nil-Rated / Zero-Rated, and the New Item quick-create never asked it - a dead end for produce. The Tax Treatment choice now appears there the moment the rate is 0 and is required and sent only then.
- [x] **66.5 No number spinners anywhere (user thumb rule).** `styles.css` hides the spin buttons on every `input[type=number]`; `app.js` `installNumberStepGuards()` (installed once with the date picker enhancer) blurs a focused number box on wheel and cancels ArrowUp/ArrowDown stepping (screens' own arrow-key handlers still run). Boxes stay `type="number"`, so only a valid number is accepted.
- Verified: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `node --check` (app.js + both modules), `TestHSNMasterIsWellFormed`, `TestHSNWordsMatching`, `TestHSNMasterRanking` (41 cases incl. zircon / silver / german silver / gold plated / cloth vs fabric vs garment, muffler by industry), `TestSuggestHSNForTenant` (throwaway tenant: industry seeding, own-item history beats the master, HistoryDoctypes gate, code/word search, price-banded rates, HSN-list rate listed first), `TestIndustryLockAndOverride`, full `internal/server` (-p 1), `internal/securityscan`. Chrome on a scratch server against local :5490: New Item quick-create "zircon haar" -> 711719 first, GST 3 filled; 620520 clears it and offers 5% / 18% with notes; "imitation" search; ArrowUp and wheel do not step; 0% shows Tax Treatment, blocks submit until chosen, payload carries it (POST intercepted, nothing created); full Item form "Cotton Bedsheet Double" -> 630221, no rate filled, chip fills 5. Cold core: this stage **-225 bytes** gzip (app.js -313, styles.css +88; measured HEAD + only these hunks; the attachTypeahead header comments were condensed). `app.js?v=46`, `styles.css?v=30`.

Fix it."* The Item field `variant_option_values` reached the studio under its form label "Variant Options (Key:Value;Key:Value)", its sample value was that same label (text fields fell back to their label), and a real print put the stored shorthand (`metal_type:Silver;size:7`) on the tag. Now: `engines.StickerVariantText` turns it into the values only (**Silver / 7**) inside `stickerItemFields`, the one place every print path (QZ/ZPL and the browser sheet) gets Item fields from; `sticker-engine.js` `variantText` mirrors it for the studio's real-item preview; the drawer calls the field **Variant** with a **Silver / 7** sample. ## Stage 67 — Stay signed in for a 12-hour shift; grouped Setup and Settings menus (opened + built 2026-10-17 from the user's login-screen screenshot)

User, 2026-10-17, bounced to the login screen from Approvals: *"SIgningout is fast. I want to stay 12 hr not get logged out."*, then with screenshots of both flyouts: *"Need grouping of setup and setting & 12hr deafult unless user changes."*

Cause: the server token lives 24 h (`security.session_token_ttl_hours`), but every user was seeded with a 30-minute client-side idle auto-logout (`security.default_idle_timeout_minutes`, longest choice 2 h). Each tab also ran its own timer while `logout()` clears the one shared `localStorage` token, so an ERP tab idle in the background signed out the tab being worked in.

- [x] **67.1 Activity shared across tabs.** `app.js` idle timer: `noteIdleActivity()` writes the last-activity time to `localStorage.erp_last_activity` (at most one write per 5 s); `armIdleTimer()` signs out only once the newest activity from any tab is older than the limit, else re-arms for the remainder. Listeners now capture (passive) and include `wheel`/`touchstart`, so scrolling inside a table or panel (scroll does not bubble) counts.
- [x] **67.2 12 hours, the default unless the user changes it.** New `720` ("12 hours") in the Profile select (`IDLE_TIMEOUT_OPTIONS`), the server allow-list (`handlers_profile.go`) and the setting's options; the setting's default is now 720. `engines/saas.go` seeds a new client's first admin from the setting too (it took the column's 30 before). `db/migrations_stage67_2_idle_timeout_12h.sql`, every `tenant_*` schema: column default 720, and users still at the old 30 seed move to 720. A user on any other value keeps it; a tenant whose admin explicitly set the default (`system_settings` row) is skipped. Ledger-tracked, runs once, so a later choice of 30 sticks.
- [x] **67.3 Grouped Settings and Setup menus.** Settings flyout (`index.html`) gets four `submenu-group-label` headings - Users & Access / Customize / Monitoring / Platform - with entries regrouped, ids unchanged; `syncFlyoutHeadings()` hides a heading with nothing visible under it. Setup was already grouped by module (30.5.4) but showed flat: `syncFlyoutHeadings()` (Stage 57) meant to skip Setup but named a non-existent `#menu-master-definition-submenu`, then judged every Setup heading empty (its entries are `.submenu-item`, not `.menu-item`) and hid it. The guard now names `#submenu-master`.

## Stage 69 — Create and history on separate pages, everywhere: list first, + New opens the form as its own page (opened + built 2026-10-17 from the user's HR Attendance screenshot)

User, 2026-10-17, on HR → Attendance (form above the records): *"& All txn where there is create and history both will be different page. PO, grn all TO TO Stock movement All"*, then *"Make it standard practice how it is in best erp being followed"*. Taken as the Odoo / ERPNext / Zoho / Fiori shape: a record **opens on its list** (count beside the title), **+ New …** top right opens the form as a page of its own with a breadcrumb back, browser Back / Forward walk between the two, saving returns to the list. Stage 65.8 / 68's New / History tabs (New as the landing page) are replaced by this on every screen that had them. POS keeps its Sale / Return tabs (a till opens ready to sell). Generic record lists (Setup masters, the HR tabs that are doctype tables, Vendor / Sales Invoice, Debit / Credit Notes) already had list + **New** and are unchanged.

- [x] **69.1 One shared mechanism (`public/txn-screen.js`, outside the cold core).** A screen opts in with `data-landing="<list pane>"`. Each page is a browser history entry on the **same address** (`history.pushState({txnPane})`), so Back leaves New for the list without the hash changing - the router's `hashchange` never fires, nothing re-renders, half-typed work survives - and `history.state` is the one record of which page shows: a re-render on a New entry (a setup detour returning, a refresh) comes back on New. `txnShowPane` (push / replace / `history.back()`), one `popstate` listener, `txnPageOf`, `txnRestorePane` (paged: the page the entry names; others: the last pane, as before), `txnDone` (a save returns to the list; `forceLanding` covers the re-render that races the async `back()`), `txnOnPage` (a screen that re-renders per page - the PO), `txnPageTitle` (list: h1 + count; New: breadcrumb › page title; the list h1 stays first and keeps `.page-title` for the "Back to …" detour link), `txnNewButton`, `txnPagedSection` (wraps an ordinary page's form panel + list panel - and several forms over one list, My Requests / Manufacturing). The module loads `txn-screen.css` itself when a view's registry entry names none (awaited, no unstyled paint) - `app.js` untouched, cold core +0. CSS (`txn-screen.css`): `.txn-landing-only` / `.txn-page-only` keyed to the screen's own bar only (`screen > bar > part`), so a paged section inside another's page (an RFQ's quotes) keeps its own state; `.txn-count`, `.txn-crumb`, `.page-section-bar`.
- [x] **69.2 Stage 68 screens moved to list-first:** Stock Transfer, Journal Vouchers, Goods Receipt, Purchase Return (in place - the work lives in the page), Purchase Order (re-renders per page via `txnOnPage`; `poPage` now comes from the history entry; **+ New Purchase Order** drops an unsaved Edit for a blank PO, keeps a half-typed new one; Edit = the same page, breadcrumb *Purchase Orders › Edit PO/…*). **Receive goods** on a PO opens the GRN's New page with the PO loaded.

The form panels' own h2 titles went (the breadcrumb names the page). Payroll Export is a report, unchanged.

## Stage 70 — Sticker templates: variant on the standard label, duplicate the standard, template rules, template chosen at print (opened + built 2026-10-17 from the user's message after 66.6)

User, 2026-10-17: *"set on std barcode template also. i should be able to copy and create duplicate of template need a rule setup of barcode and in txn need a option to make active"*. Asked two questions; answers: the rule decides **which template prints** (Item → Item type → category → default, as batch numbers and barcodes do), and "in txn" is a **template picker at print**, for the whole run plus per line, for that print only. Numbered 70 because Stages 68 and 69 belong to the concurrent transaction-screen session.

Starter button renamed **Use the standard label (50 × 25)**. Duplicate of a stored template was already there (58.2).
- [x] **70.3 Template rules.** Migration `db/migrations_stage70_1_sticker_template_rules.sql`: `Item.sticker_template` and `ItemType.sticker_template` (Link → StickerTemplate, blank = next rule, so nothing changes until set). `engines/stickers.go`: `pickStickerTemplate` (pure rule order: Item, Item type, category, default, standard; a rule naming an Inactive/deleted template is skipped), `ResolveStickerTemplateForItem` (returns the rule that decided), `ChosenStickerTemplate`, `activeStickerTemplates` now `ORDER BY id` (two templates on one category / two defaults were scan-order dependent - the ADMIN_GUIDE pitfall) and `deleted_at IS NULL` (**found:** a soft-deleted template still printed). `ItemTypeRules.StickerTemplate` via the item-type cache. New tab **Sticker Printing → Template rules** (`renderStickerTemplateRules` in `sticker-studio.js`): item count per template (rule 1, set on the Item form), Item type table, Category table (+ "Another category"; a category is moved off every other template so it has one owner), Default dropdown (ticks one, clears the rest), Duplicate the standard label. Saves through the generic doc API with `expected_version`. No subtitle line (67.4).
- [x] **70.4 Template chosen at print.** `StickerLineSelection.TemplateID` (`template_id` in `lines`; a template id or `standard`) carried by `filterStickerLines` into `resolveAndLogSticker`, which uses it before the rules; a non-Active choice stops the run (422, message names the template). Preview lines carry `template_id` + `template_rule`. GRN / Transfer Order print table: **Template** column is a dropdown (first option "<rule's template> (item / item type / category / default / no rule)", then Standard label and every Active template) and **Template for every line** above it ("Chosen per line" when they differ). `sticker_print_log.template_id` records what printed. Each becomes printable once its module item in 71.7 exists (the print payload already carries every header field).
- [ ] **71.7 Modules the pack needs that the ERP does not have** - **now tracked as Stage 74** (2026-10-17: the user's *Transaction Modules Master BRD* specifies all eleven below; build order = its waves, open decisions = 74.0.1). Kept here as the original list:
  - (a) **Delivery Challan** - standalone goods movement without an invoice (job work, sample, repair, exhibition), returnable / non-returnable with expected return, consignor / consignee / place of supply, copy labels Original / Duplicate / Triplicate. Today only a same-GSTIN Transfer Out prints as a challan.
  - (b) **Customer Advance Receipt** - own number series, tender + payment ref, balance, later redemption against a sale with the advance referenced. Nothing exists (POS tenders are Cash / Card / UPI).
  - (c) **Internal Material Issue / Receipt** - consumables / packaging to a department or cost centre with a stock movement and cost posting. Only production material issue exists.
  - (d) **Inventory Adjustment** as its own document - one-off gain / loss / damage / write-off with a reason code, maker-checker, value impact, GL posting and reversal. Today adjustments come only from cycle counts.
  - (e) **Job Work Outward / Return** - upgrade `SubcontractOrder` (one SKU, one receipt) to many lines, gross / net weight, partial returns, balance with the job worker, loss / variance approval, challan print.
  - (f) **Store credit / credit voucher** - a sales return refunded as credit the customer redeems later. Today every refund books cash.
  - (g) **E-invoice (IRN, signed QR) and e-way bill** - fields plus the NIC / GSP integration. Deliberately not built so far.
  - (h) **Document print log** - print version, reprint count and reprint reason per document print (stickers already log reprints).
  - (i) **Reason codes everywhere** - the `ReasonCode` master exists, but GRN rejection / shortage, purchase return, transfer discrepancy and reprint take free text.
  - (j) **PO commercial terms** and **GRN logistics fields** (71.6 lists them) on the PO and GRN screens.
  - (k) **Print profiles** - company logo setting, copy labels, template-driven POS receipt profiles (B2C / B2B / exchange / gift); Sales Invoice customer GSTIN / place of supply and a real entry screen.
- [x] **71.8 Multi-page prints: header on the first page only, tax / totals / signatures at the end** (user, 2026-10-17: *"if I have two pages, the header should not come and bottom of text tax part and all should come in the end ... header should not come except 1st page"*). Reproduced first in a renderer harness: the standard layouts printed brand / title / number / divider on **every** page (`pages:'all'`), and a template saved from the studio printed a terms line and rule added under the table on **page 1** (palette items default to "First page"); page 1 also stopped at the footer line (218 mm) leaving ~70 mm blank. Fixed once in the renderer (`public/trade-docs.js`), so it covers the built-in layouts **and every saved template** without touching stored layouts: with an items table, **position decides** - `pagesByPosition` / `printsOn` put an element whose centre is above the table on the first page only and one below it on the last page only; a page number and anything beside the table keep their own **Prints on**. `tableBox` gives later pages the top margin and pages before the last the bottom margin (2 mm clear of a page number); `paginate` fills each page and moves to the last page once the rows left fit above its footer, handing one row on when a page would otherwise take every row and leave the totals alone. Column headings still repeat on every page. The auto-added brand prints on the first page only. `contY` ("Later pages start at Y") is retired - the input is removed from the studio, stored values are ignored. Studio: the canvas paints the one design page with `renderPages(..., { design: true })` (every element, the designed box), element tags show the effective page, and **Prints on** is shown disabled ("First page (above the items table)" / "Last page (below the items table)") where position decides; hint text updated. USER_GUIDE §7B.2 steps 2 and 4, ADMIN_GUIDE print section (brand, new **Multi-page documents** note). **Verified:** `node --check` both files; harness (Chrome / Playwright over `public/`, before vs after): PO standard layout at 5 / 30 / 38 / 80 rows and a studio-style template at 30 rows - after the fix page 2+ carry no brand / title / number / divider, the table starts at 10 mm, pages before the last run to 286 mm (38 rows on page 1 instead of 25), the tax summary, totals, words, signatures, audit line and the studio-added terms / rule print on the last page only, page number on every page, a 5-row PO is unchanged on one page. **Live** (scratch :8177, local DB on 5490, print data stretched to 40 lines by route interception, no data written): QA-PO-002 printed 3 pages through the real print dialog with exactly that split; **Design templates** opened the studio with the full design page (header, table, totals), tags "first page" / "last page" / "every page", **Prints on** disabled for brand and totals, editable for the page number, no "Later pages start at Y"; no page or console errors. **Not committed, not deployed** (frontend only - no migration).

## Stage 72 — Item & Material Master: independent classification, sales-triggered backflush at the POS, GWP, gift cards as stored value (opened + built 2026-10-17 from the user's two PDFs)

**Why it was opened.** The user shared *Backflush vs Non-Sellable Items in Retail ERP/POS* (10 pp.) with *"Build this also for pos"* - Stage 60.4's Backflush item type only consumed components at production completion. Asked whether gift cards were in scope, they answered *"Wait. I will reshare"* and then sent the full 41-page *Enterprise Item & Material Master Module - Build Blueprint* (v1.0, 17 Oct 2026, built from the first PDF): *"Read and update checklist and build"*. Its one rule: *"Do not build Asset, Non-Asset, Gift, Backflush, Voucher and Sellable as one mutually exclusive item-type list"* - every item answers six independent questions (class, stock tracking, valuation, sellability, consumption, accounting); behaviour comes from profiles + rule tables, never from a named item.

**Decisions.** User, 2026-10-17: **short packaging stock never blocks the sale** - the default short-stock action is *Allow and report* (Block / Warn stay selectable per rule, blueprint UAT-10). Taken without asking (reversible, say if wrong): the Stage 60 **Item Type master is the blueprint's reusable profile** (section 5, "many items -> 1 profile") rather than six new profile masters, with item-level overrides - one place to configure, the existing pattern; the seven Stage 60 types keep today's behaviour except **Gift and Free With Item become Promo only** (the till could not bill a zero-priced line before this stage anyway - see 72.2); a gift's cost goes to **5170 Promotional / GWP Expense**, not COGS; gift cards are issued as their **own operation with a separate receipt**, not as a bill line (blueprint 16.1 "Separate issue receipt").

**What already existed (gap analysis, 2026-10-17).** Item Type master with barcode / batch / sticker rules and four behaviour flags (60.3/60.4); Item status = generic `documents.status`, soft delete, no Item approval rule; per-key `audit_logs` trigger (records the creator, no reason); `expected_version` optimistic concurrency (409); moving-average costing only; no negative-stock setting; no service / non-stock items; `ProductBundle` Virtual / Stocked kits exploded on OMS sales orders only; GRN -> Draft Asset per unit for Fixed Asset items (57.8); no stored value (`Voucher` is a coupon), one payment mode per bill; no void of a posted bill (returns only); offers: % / flat / BxGy / bundle price, no GWP; Location.sellable only, no item x location policy.

- [x] **72.1 Eight independent dimensions on the Item Type profile, item overrides, compatibility rules** (blueprint 3, 5, 6, 15.2; FR-001/002/022; ITM-005..009). `engines/item_policy.go`: commercial class, stock tracking, valuation, accounting treatment, sellability, customer bill, POS capture, consumption + expense account; precedence Item > Item Type > what the Stage 60 flags meant > company default (ordinary merchandise), each dimension's source reported. `db/migrations_stage72_1_item_policy.sql`: the nine fields on ItemType and (with "Item type default") on Item; GL 5160 Packaging Consumption, 5170 Promotional / GWP Expense, 5180 Consumables Expense, 2610 Gift Card / Stored Value Liability; dimensions filled on the seven Stage 60 types only where blank; new profiles **Packaging, Consumable - Periodic, Service, Non-Stock Expense, Gift Card, Virtual Kit**. Save-time rules through `ValidateMasterDataRules` (UI, import and API alike): MASTER-0401 valuated needs inventory asset, 0402 tracking vs valuation, 0403 fixed-asset accounting only on Fixed Asset, 0404 stored value <-> issue-only + liability, 0405 auto/periodic consumption needs stock, 0406 expense account must exist. Production backflush now also follows `consumption = Auto backflush`.
- [x] **72.2 Sellability at the till** (14.1, FR-013/021, ITM-003/004, UAT-14/28). In `ResolvePOSQuote` (screen, checkout and price override all price through it): Not sellable -> POSOFF-0411, stored-value item -> POSOFF-0413, Promo only -> billed at 0 and allowed only up to what a Gift With Purchase offer (or its Free-With-Item parent) authorises, else POSOFF-0412. Each refused attempt logged once per cart as an Item Exception (Logged). Not-tracked lines flagged `not_stocked`; hidden-bill zero-value lines kept off both receipts (browser + ESC/POS). **Bug found and fixed:** the POS screen refused every line priced 0 ("price greater than zero"), so Stage 60.4's Gift / Free With Item could never actually be billed.
- [x] **72.3 Backflush rules and sale-triggered consumption** (9.1-9.3, FR-004/008/010, UAT-07/26). `BackflushRule` master (Setup -> Inventory, numbered BF-0001): applies to Item / Attribute (Name:Value) / Category / Item Type / All items, store (blank = all), consumed item, qty per unit, group, priority, cashier may change, reason needed, None allowed, short-stock action, effective dates. `engines/backflush_rules.go`: winner per group by 9.2 specificity (item, attribute, category, item type, all; a store's rule before the all-stores one; priority breaks ties); groups add up; only normally-sold lines trigger. Checkout resolves it from the server's own priced lines, stores it on the POSCart with rule id + version, and `finalizePOSCheckoutTx` posts it in the sale's transaction: stock out (voucher `POSConsumption`, the bill number), valuated components Dr expense / Cr 1200 at moving-average cost, non-valuated quantity only. A replay posts nothing twice. MASTER-0408 on an incomplete rule.
- [x] **72.4 POS Packaging override** (9.4, FR-009, UAT-08/09, ITM-011). The quote returns each line's default and choices; **Packaging** button (shown only when something can change) opens the shared dialog: per line x group a choice (+ None where allowed) and a reason; overrides validated server-side (POSOFF-0414 reason, POSOFF-0415 not an option). Verified live (see Verification).
- [x] **72.5 Short-stock policy and exception queue** (9.1, 18.1, FR-029, UAT-10, ITM-012). `ItemException` (Setup -> Inventory): engine-written, managers set Resolved. Block -> POSOFF-0416 before anything posts; Allow (default) and Warn post and open one exception per bill x component; the quote shows the shortfall on the till.
- [x] **72.6 Consumption Entry - periodic and manual** (9.6, FR-012, UAT-13). `engines/consumption_entry.go`, POST /api/v1/inventory/consumption (store manager / admin): periodic = book - counted closing, manual = quantity used; counted > book or used > book refused (INVENT-0422), non-stock refused (INVENT-0421); valuated -> Dr expense / Cr 1200, non-valuated quantity only; keeps count reference and approver; idempotent. Screen: **Inventory -> Consumption Entry** (store manager / admin), lazy module `public/view-consumption.js` - store, type, count reference, items with their book stock, counted or used quantity, live consumed column, confirm, post. Verified live.
- [x] **72.7 Gift With Purchase** (10, FR-014, UAT-14/15). Offer type *Gift With Purchase* (gift item, gift qty per bill or per Buy Qty units), evaluated on paid lines only, never using a discount slot; the till prompts "Gift eligible ... scan it"; the scanned gift is billed at 0 with the offer as `promotion_ref`; its cost to the gift profile's expense account. *Reports -> Gift With Purchase Register*. Verified live.
- [x] **72.8 Gift cards as stored value** (11, 15.1, FR-015..018, UAT-16..20/27, ITM-014..016). `StoredValueProfile` (seeded *Standard Gift Card*: 16 digits, 100-50,000, 12 months, part use, no reload); `stored_value_instruments` + append-only `stored_value_ledger` (trigger refuses UPDATE / DELETE, put in place lazily for clients provisioned later; data console read-only, numbers masked). Register (list or generate Luhn numbers, duplicates refused), issue / add value (funded only when the posting commits: Dr payment clearing / Cr 2610; idempotent, a replay returns the receipt), balance, redeem as a tender inside checkout (Dr 2610 / Cr clearing, capped at what is owed, never offline), block / unblock with reason, cancel an unused issue (refund to the original mode). Reports *Gift Card Liability* and *Gift Card Ledger*. POS **Gift Card** dialog: pay this bill (bar shows the card, Total drops; online only - never queued, so a number never lands in the offline store), issue / add value with a separate printed receipt, balance, remove from bill; managers: block, release, cancel unused, register (generated numbers shown once for printing). Finished bill and ESC/POS receipt print each card as a tender line. Verified live. Open from Stage 73 D08: refund-to-card and transferability rules (72.D g).
- [x] **72.9 Items that keep no stock** (8.2, 13.1, 14.1, UAT-04/05, ITM-002). POS: no stock, no COGS, no costing gap (verified). GRN: no stock, value to the profile's expense account; transfer orders refuse them (INVENT-0421); consumption refuses them. GRN and transfer paths tested (`TestStage72ReceivingTransfersAndReports`).
- [x] **72.10 Non-valuated stock** (8, 12.4, UAT-03). Quantity kept; GRN value to expense, not 1200, never in the moving average; issued at no cost (POS, consumption - verified). GRN path tested; *Reports -> Non-Valuated Stock*.
- [x] **72.11 Virtual kits at the till** (13.2, FR-020, UAT-21). A Virtual `ProductBundle` sold at the POS issues its components and costs them (verified); the parent keeps no stock.
- [x] **72.12 Treatment frozen after stock history** (7.2, UAT-24, ITM-018). MASTER-0407 on an Item or Item Type change of stock tracking / valuation / accounting once stock ledger history exists.
- [x] **72.13 Reports** (20), all in the Report Catalog: Item Classification Register, Item Master Completeness, Backflush Consumption, Gift With Purchase Register, Non-Valuated Stock, Inactive Items With Stock, Item Exceptions, Gift Card Liability, Gift Card Ledger (`engines/item_master_reports.go`, `stored_value.go`). Not built from section 20: Item Master Change Log (needs 72.D e), Backflush Variance, Non-Stock Spend, Valuated Inventory (the existing Inventory Valuation covers it), Fixed Asset Acquisition Candidates (57.8 raises the Draft Assets directly), Negative Stock Exception (the Item Exceptions short-stock rows cover packaging).
- [x] **72.14 Guides, UAT checklist, brain map, attack surface.** USER_GUIDE section 4.6 (packaging, gifts, gift cards, items the till will not sell), section 5 step 4 (Consumption Entry), section 6.8 rewritten (item types as profiles); ADMIN_GUIDE section B.3.8 (dimensions, rules, shipped profiles, Backflush Rules, Gift With Purchase, gift cards, Consumption Entry, exceptions and reports, open decisions) with a pointer from the Stage 60 Item Types section; UAT_CHECKLIST section 25 (UAT-05..28 for the till); brain map claims the eight new Go files (redraw 100%); attack surface regenerated (527 routes, `-check` current).

A new lazy module `public/view-pos-extras.js` (same `?v=`, imported as soon as the till renders) now holds the Return tab (filled into the empty `#pos-return-pane` the till makes), + New Customer, Check / Redeem Points, and the Packaging and Gift Card dialogs. The till's own state reaches them through one accessor object (`posTill`); `updatePOSReturnLine` is published on `window` for the data-act dispatcher. No behaviour change. Cold core 183,937 / 184,320. Verified: `node --check`; Chrome (scratch :8171, local DB, Store Manager): the extras module loads with `?v=46`, the Return pane is hidden on Sale, Check / Redeem Points answer with no customer, the + New Customer prompt opens, the Gift Card dialog shows the 7 manager actions, Balance hides Amount and an unknown card gets the gift-card error, the Return tab looks up a paid cart, its quantity box (data-act) enables Raise Return, there is one Return pane after leaving and re-entering POS, and no page errors.
