## Stage 73 — Enterprise suite completion blueprint: the 346-row gap register mapped onto the build (external PDF; opened 2026-10-17 · Waves 1-3 and 73.J built 2026-10-17/18, released 2026-10-18 (73.R) · pending list below)

User, 2026-10-17: *"read and plan and update in checklist"*, sharing `Enterprise_Business_Platform_Suite_Completion_Blueprint.pdf` (v1.0, 41 pages, outside the repo at `C:\Users\ABCD\Downloads\MyBusiness\IT Solution\ERP\Docs\PDF\`). It benchmarks the suite against SAP S/4HANA and Dynamics 365 process areas: §1 assessment and product boundaries (PIM, OMS, WMS, HRMS, CRM/Loyalty and POS as first-class products, ERP as the financial backbone), §3 fifteen end-to-end processes, §4 ten design principles, §5 the P0/P1 go-live gaps, §6 the loyalty target design, §7 twenty-seven module blueprints each with a completion gate, §8 twelve policy decisions (D01-D12), §9 seven delivery waves, §10 ten acceptance journeys (J1-J10), §11 the Definition of Done, §12 the minimum configuration catalogue, §13 a leadership scorecard, and §14 a 346-row capability register (167 Built-verify, 85 Partial, 77 Missing / benchmark gap, 14 External gate, 3 Optional / scale-gated).

**Pending (2026-10-18) - everything else in this stage is `[x]`. 55 open items, grouped by who acts:**
- **Your decisions:** 73.0.1 confirm D04 / D05 / D06 as built (D10 when SSO comes up); 73.0.2 capability register - all 346 rows or the 179; 73.B.1 fractional BOM quantity at material issue (round up / round / refuse); 73.C.11 risky-rights flags on Stage 79's Roles screen; 73.D.1 depreciation dated at month-end (built that way - confirm); 73.D.2 whether an employee may read back their own grievance; 73.4.1-73.4.10 Wave 4 scope (nothing started).
- **You run:** nothing - 73.D.3 was regenerated at the 2026-10-18 release (73.R).
- **Fixes to build, highest risk first:** money / tax 73.D.10 (credit notes leave output GST booked - overstates the GST return), 73.D.11, 73.D.13, 73.D.15, 73.D.16, 73.D.12; access 73.D.8, 73.D.7, 73.D.6, 73.D.9, 73.D.17, 73.D.20; stock 73.B.8, 73.B.6; smaller 73.B.7, 73.B.10, 73.C.7, 73.C.8, 73.C.12, 73.D.14, 73.D.18, 73.D.19, 73.D.23, 73.D.24; tests 73.B.9, 73.D.21, 73.C.9.
- **Release steps:** all done at the 2026-10-18 release (73.R): 73.C.6 → 73.C.2 → 73.B.5 → 73.D.5 → 73.C.3 → 73.C.4 → 73.B.4.
- **On site / sign-offs:** 73.B.11 RF scanner floor run; 73.C.13 Stage 55 Done-bar walks; 55.10 Account Head + CA review and 55.14 HR policy-owner sign-off (73.D's header).
- **Dev data cleanup (ask first):** 73.B.13, 73.C.5.

**How this stage is built (read before adding to it):**
- **An index, not a copy.** The PDF's own backlog rule: *"every implementation ticket should reference one capability row ... do not create another parallel master checklist"*. A row an open item already owns points there (→ 59.3); only rows nobody owns get a 73.x item. Its wave order (§9) is the build order: *"do not start a new breadth module while P0 economic correctness is still open"*.
- **Every Partial / Missing claim was re-checked against source on 2026-10-17**, because the PDF's evidence was older docs (handover, an older copy of this file, the capability catalogue). Two read-only sweeps, ~160 claims, file:line evidence in each item; the two P0 claims that open new items on their own (73.1.1, 73.1.9) were also read directly. Where the PDF is wrong: 73.0.3.
- **The 167 Built-verify rows are not listed one by one.** Their acceptance tests run through the journeys (73.J) and the existing 47.17.1 / 50.17 / 26.11.5 work; they are proven, not rebuilt.
- **Missing / benchmark-gap rows are scope decisions, not a build order** (standing rule: no new breadth without the user's go-ahead). Wave 4 lists them as input to 50.18's domain-expansion decision.
- **Numbered 73, not 72:** a concurrent session's **Stage 72** (the user's Item & Material Master blueprint - item policy dimensions, backflush rules, consumption entry, item exception queue, stored-value gift cards / vouchers; `db/migrations_stage72_*.sql`) had no section here when this stage opened; it has one now and shipped in 0845470. Where that work answers a row (gift cards, item taxonomy, a data-quality queue) this stage points at it rather than duplicating it.
- **Acceptance bar for every 73.x item is the PDF's §11 Definition of Done:** normal, partial, cancel, retry and reversal paths work; stock, tax, money and points post exactly once; rules are enforced server-side; lineage and audit exist; an authorised novice can finish the task; reports reconcile; the guides describe what shipped.

### 73.0 Wave 0 — decisions and baseline

- [ ] **73.0.1 Policy decisions D01-D12** **[needs design decision: user - only D04, D05 and D06 (confirm as built) and D10 remain]**. PDF §8: building without them hard-codes accidental behaviour. The PDF's recommended default is in brackets, followed by where things stand today:
  - **D01 Loyalty on return** [reverse earned points pro-rata on the returned eligible value] - blocks 73.1.2. **Decided by the user 2026-10-17: the default above.**
  - **D02 Loyalty refund** [the redeemed share goes back as points at the original allocation; money only for the money-funded share; no cash conversion] - blocks 73.1.3. **Decided by the user 2026-10-17: the default above.**
  - **D03 Negative points** when the earned points are already spent [business call: allow a negative / recovery balance, or cap at zero and flag the debt] - blocks 73.1.2. **Decided by the user 2026-10-17: allow a negative balance, paid back by later earnings; the cash refund is never cut (built in 73.1.2).**
  - **D04 Transfer approval** [independent maker-checker, or direct approval for low-risk internal transfers] - blocks 73.1.7. **Built to this default in 73.1.7** (direct approval below `inventory.transfer_direct_approval_limit`, maker-checker above); no user confirmation recorded - confirm.
  - **D05 Rejected GRN quantity** [stays open on the PO for replacement, or counts as fulfilled with a claim / debit note] - blocks 73.2.4. **Built in 73.2.4:** rejected / damaged units stay open on the PO for replacement; short-close needs a reason and raises a claim. No user confirmation recorded - confirm.
  - **D06 Vendor quote basis** [unit / line / total price; UOM, tax, freight, lead time and award fields] - blocks 73.2.2. **Built in 73.2.2:** per-line unit price, quoted UOM converted to the RFQ unit, tax %, freight, terms and validity, compared on landed total. No user confirmation recorded - confirm.
  - **D07 Mixed-owner WMS** - **decided 2026-10-08: full isolation, built under Stage 59** (59.1-59.6 all closed; archiving the stage is 73.B.4).
  - **D08 Gift card / store credit** [separate liabilities, refund rules, expiry, transferability] - gift cards built in 72.8 (deployed, 0845470); store credit 71.7(f); refund-to-card and transferability rules are open as 72.D(g).
  - **D09 GL correction policy** [which posted documents may be reversed, reopened or adjusted; who may override a closed period] - today a closed period has no reopen, by design (`engines/accounting_periods.go`); blocks 73.1.10. **Decided 2026-10-17 (user): the Accountant role approves a reopen; built in 73.1.10.**
  - **D10 SSO / SCIM** [only if target enterprise customers require it, local identity otherwise] - go-to-market call; 73.4.9.
  - **D11 Data mart / read replica** - already gated by 26.10.6's measured thresholds.
  - **D12 Hosting / WAF / DR** - partly decided (Stage 56, Railway Pro, 2026-10-04); WAF 26.1.3b, alerts 20.2 and hosting 20.4 still open.

### 73.1 Wave 1 — P0 economic correctness (first)

- [x] **73.1.1 A POS return appears to refund the full line price, ignoring the bill-level offer discount and redeemed points: reproduce, then fix.** `resolveOriginalSaleLinePrices` (`engines/returns.go:86-130`) refunds each line at its stored `sale_price`. The offer discount is bill-level (`pos_quote.go:389-396`, `Total = Subtotal - OfferDiscount`) and so is loyalty redemption (`pos_checkout.go:234-256`); neither is stored per line, and `returns.go` reads neither. Returning one line of a discounted or points-funded bill therefore likely over-refunds, in cash. **First** a failing test (offer bill + redeemed points, return one line, assert refund = that line's net share). **Then** persist the per-line allocation on the sale at checkout - offer share, points and their money value, tender share, a deterministic rounding remainder (PDF §6 "Partial return allocation", §6.1 step 2) - and refund from it. Foundation for 73.1.2, 73.1.3 and 73.1.6. [rows: Sale return P0, Partial return allocation P0] **Built 2026-10-17 (Batch A) - and the sale side was worse than this item says:** the offer discount never reached the GL or the tax. Every offer sale credited 4100 and debited cash at the list total and backed GST out of the list price (reproduced: 3,000 revenue / 2,730 cash / 457.62 GST on a bill that collected 2,430). Now `EvaluatePOSOffers` splits each offer across its lines (`LineDiscounts`, largest remainder by what is left of each line); the quote carries `offer_share` / `net_total` per line and backs GST out of the net; finalize posts the net (`PostSalesFinanceBookingTx` accepts a wholly free bill), caps points at the net in points not rupees, earns on the money paid, and stores the per-line `allocation` (offer, points, gift card, each money tender) on the cart (`engines/sale_allocation.go`). A return prices its units from that allocation under the sale's lock, cumulatively (`unitShare`; the net is the sum of the tender parts so a refund journal always balances), so a line returned in pieces refunds exactly what it collected; a bill sold before this rebuilds its allocation from what it recorded. **Also fixed:** `RedeemLoyaltyPointsTx` ran `FOR UPDATE` on an aggregate, so every till sale that redeemed points failed (Postgres 0A000) - now a per-customer transaction advisory lock (`lockedLoyaltyBalanceTx`). Receipt prints the posted points; POS Sale Reconciliation holds sales to the net, with tenders measured net. **Historic sales are not re-posted** (release data-safety rule): past offer sales will now read BROKEN in POS Sale Reconciliation, which is accurate - finance decides whether to journal the difference. Tests `TestStage73POSReturnAllocation`, `TestStage73AllocationArithmetic`, `TestStage73RedemptionCapReturnsPoints`; live Chrome on scratch :8201 (throwaway client): 2,700 net, GST on 2,700.
- [x] **73.1.2 Reverse earned points on a return.** `ApplyReturnQC` / `ProcessRefundRequest` never touch `loyalty_point_ledger`. Reverse pro-rata on the returned line's original eligible value (from 73.1.1's snapshot) as an append-only Reversal entry - never an edit - applying D03's policy when the points are already spent. Needs D01, D03. [row: Return earn reversal P0] **Built 2026-10-17 (D01 + D03 decided by the user 2026-10-17: pro rata on the money returned; a balance may go negative and is paid back by later earnings, the cash refund is never cut).** `reverseEarnedPointsTx` (`engines/loyalty_returns.go`), in the refund's transaction under the customer's loyalty lock: cumulative target (the sale's earn x money refunded so far / the sale's money) less what earlier refunds reversed, as an append-only `Reversal` ledger row naming the refund. Tested: 8 then 16 of 24 points over two returns; spent-then-returned -> balance -9 with the full cash refund.
- [x] **73.1.3 Restore redeemed points on a return: points, not cash.** `ReverseLoyaltyRedemption` (`loyalty.go:174`) exists, but only tests call it. Refund the loyalty-funded share as points at the original allocation; restored points keep their original expiry or a configured minimum grace, so a same-day return never loses them (PDF §6 "Expiry after restoration"). Needs D02. [row: Return redeem restore P0] **Built 2026-10-17 (D02 decided by the user 2026-10-17).** `restoreRedeemedPointsTx`: the returned units' points share (from the allocation) goes back as an `Earn` row on the refund, expiring at the latest expiry the customer's points had at the sale, never sooner than new setting `loyalty.restored_points_grace_days` (30) from the return; GL credits 5250 for their value. Tested: 90 restored valid 30 days; a wholly returned bill leaves the balance where it started. Live: 90 restored to 2026-11-16.
- [x] **73.1.4 Redemption controls on the till path.** The till calls `RedeemLoyaltyPointsTx` (`pos_checkout.go:236`), which checks only the balance (`loyalty.go:67`); the OTP and velocity checks live only on `/crm/loyalty-redemption`. Move them into the shared redemption function - one choke point, so till, API and offline replay all enforce them - plus manager approval above a configured value, and reason codes. [row: POS redemption fraud P0] **Built 2026-10-17.** One choke point: `RedeemLoyaltyPointsTx` -> `authorizeLoyaltyRedemptionTx`, under the customer's lock: the daily limit, the one-time code (new setting `loyalty.redemption_requires_otp`, off by default) and the LoyaltyRedemptionRequest approval slab (500+ Store Manager as shipped), each spent by its one redemption (`consumed_at` on the challenge, `db/migrations_stage73_1_4_redemption_controls.sql`; the request -> Redeemed). The `/loyalty/redeem` API, the OTP flow and an approved API request all pass through it. Till: `POST /api/v1/pos/loyalty/redemption/start` + `/verify`, `GET .../status`; a till request is approved, not burned, by the approval hook - the sale burns it. Reason codes: new category *Loyalty Redemption*. Codes CUSTOM-0601/0602/0603. Tested `TestStage73RedemptionControls` (approval spent once, code bound to its cart and spent once, limit, API refused without authorisation); live: the till raised a request with a reason and named it.

Add redeemable points, value per point, the cap and the resulting total / change due to `ResolvePOSQuote`, render the screen from it, and refuse a checkout whose points value differs from the quote. [row: Redemption value UI P0] **Built 2026-10-17.** `QuoteRequest.redeem_points`; `QuoteResult.loyalty` (balance, value per point, max points = min(balance, net / value), the capped points, their value, what the controls will ask for) and `amount_due`. The points are in the signed quote version, so a changed point value is a price change at checkout; checkout re-quotes with the points and stores the capped figure. The till renders from it - no more 1 point = Rs 1. Live: *1 point = 1 off ... up to 1000*, Total 2,700 -> 2,430.

Expected cash = float + cash tendered in sales (from the 73.1.1 allocation) - cash refunds processed while a session was open at the store the goods came back to (stamped `pos_session` on the refund) + paid in - paid out - safe drops (`SessionCashPosition`, `GET /api/v1/pos/session/summary`). Close above `pos.drawer_variance_tolerance`: reason required as before, then the session goes **Pending Approval** through the new POSSession approval rule (0+ Store Manager) - approved -> Closed, rejected -> Open for a recount (`finishPOSSessionApproval` hook). Shift handover: `POST /api/v1/pos/session/takeover` closes the open session against the next cashier's count and opens theirs with it as float, each naming the other. **Also fixed:** the till never asked for the variance reason, so a cashier whose count was over the tolerance could not close the session at all - the close flow (now in view-pos-extras.js) asks for it. `db/migrations_stage73_2_7_till_cash_movements.sql`; codes POSOFF-0607/0608. Tested `TestStage73TillCashMovements` (1000 + 500 - 500 + 200 - 50 - 1000 = 150; reject reopens, approve closes; handover links); live: More -> Cash in / out, Paid Out 50 -> 1850 expected, a close 1350 short asked why and went Pending Approval. **OMS (journey J4)**
- [x] **73.2.13 Notifications with a real delivery state.** `NotificationLog` writes "Sent" fire-and-forget (`notifications.go:136`). Send through the outbox with attempts, retry and a final state from the provider - never "Sent" without evidence. [row: Notifications P1] *Done 2026-10-17 (batch C): the log row is written **Queued** in the same transaction as a `notification_delivery` job (async_jobs is the outbox's delivery stage; `integration_event_outbox` was not used because its payload is shown on Integration Logs and fanned out to webhook subscribers, and a body can hold an OTP). The job POSTs with `delivery_id`/`X-Notification-Delivery`, SSRF-checks the URL, retries network/408/429/5xx (**Retrying**), stops at **Failed** on the last attempt (Replay resends), never re-sends one the provider has (**Accepted**/**Delivered**), and takes the provider's own delivered/failed answer; a later receipt arrives on `POST /api/public/v1/notifications/{id}/delivery-status` (new scope `notifications:write`; NOTIFI-0461..0463). Every step is appended to the row's history; old "Sent" rows stay as legacy. Migration `migrations_stage73_2_13_notification_delivery.sql`. `TestNotificationDeliveryStateFromProviderEvidence` (4 subtests) + sandbox regression + module-manifest public-route tests pass.*

- [ ] **73.D.6 `GET /api/v1/approval/log` has no permission or scope check** - any signed-in role reads any document's approval history (reject reasons included) given its id.
- [ ] **73.D.7 Writes are not self-scope-checked.** The generic document write and approval submit let an Employee Self-Service or Cashier user create / update another employee's Leave or Grievance by typing their code.
- [ ] **73.D.8 Generic save replaces the whole record.** Fields the role cannot see, and server-owned keys on posted invoices (FX stamps, GST split, settlement / payment history, outstanding), are wiped by a generic-form save; `total_amount` can change after posting. Freeze posted documents or merge server keys at the one choke point.
- [ ] **73.D.9 Report delivery secrets exposed.** Setup → Scheduled Report / Dashboard Digest (and `GET /api/v1/doc/...`) show every owner's full recipient emails and webhook URLs to any role that can read them; the outbox log prints full webhook URLs (with tokens) and emails.
- [ ] **73.D.10 Credit notes do not reverse output GST.** `PostCreditNote` debits 4150 with the whole note including GST; 2200-2202 booked by `PostSalesInvoice` stays, overstating the GST return.
- [ ] **73.D.11 TDS payment ignores currency.** `PayVendorInvoiceWithTDS` posts the raw `invoice_amount` for a foreign-currency bill.
- [ ] **73.D.12 Cash-flow forecast misses supplier bills** - its outflows filter VendorInvoice `Approved`; open bills are `Matched`.
- [ ] **73.D.13 `MatchVendorInvoice` has no status guard** - it can flip a Paid bill back to Matched / MismatchHold (ledger is protected, status is not).
- [ ] **73.D.14 `handlePostDebitNote` loses error codes** (`writeAPIErrorGeneric` instead of `writeEngineError`).
- [ ] **73.D.15 Asset Disposed without GL?** The workflow table allows Capitalised → Disposed, so a generic status change may dispose an asset with no entry - verify and block.
- [ ] **73.D.16 Backdated journal vouchers land in the posting month.** The voucher date reaches only the closed-period check; the GL row's `created_at` is the posting moment.
- [ ] **73.D.17 Role-template "global scopes" are descriptive only.** HR Manager claims HR's self-scope exemption but only Store Manager has it, so an HR Manager sees only their own grievances / appraisals.
- [ ] **73.D.18 Schedule double-send window.** The hourly report / digest worker does not lock rows, so a Run now landing on the same tick as a due schedule could send twice.
- [ ] **73.D.19 Local timestamps labelled UTC.** `timestamp without time zone` columns scan as UTC and display +5:30 where Postgres runs `Asia/Calcutta` (the dev machine; e.g. `GetReportPerformance`). Check production's Postgres time zone; fix at the read choke point if it is not UTC.
- [ ] **73.D.20 One object-level read rule.** It exists three times - inline in `handleGenericDoc`, `purchaseReturnInScope` (skips the legacy location rule and supplier scoping) and `documentReadRule` (attachments). Merge into one.

## Stage 74 — Transaction modules master BRD: the eleven 71.7 modules specified (external PDF; opened + planned 2026-10-17, nothing built yet)

User, 2026-10-17: *"Read this and prepare the checklist for this as well"*, sharing `Enterprise_Transaction_Modules_Master_BRD.pdf` (BRD-TXN-001 v1.0, 17 Oct 2026, 46 pages, same folder as the Stage 73 PDF). Contents: §1 decision brief and the eleven gaps with P0 / P1, §2 system ownership, shared lifecycle and invariants, §3 configuration masters, §4.1-4.11 one mini-BRD per module (journey, fields, numbered FR with acceptance proof, stock / GL / tax outcome, UI brief, negative scenarios, reports, test fixture), §5 posting blueprints and refund-vs-credit-note separation, §6 print outputs, §7 roles and segregation of duties, §8 command pattern, data model and illustrative APIs, §9 screen map, §10 UAT matrix (68 module cases + E2E-01..16), §11 sixteen registers R-TX-01..16 and the reconciliation identities, §12 NFR-01..15, §13 decisions DEC-01..22, §14 waves 0-5 and pilot gates, §15 statutory sources, §16 sign-off.

**How this stage is built (read before adding to it):**
- **It is 71.7, specified.** The BRD's eleven gaps are 71.7(a)-(k) item for item (its §15.1 baseline is this file and the handover). 71.7 asked which to build and in what order; the BRD gives the order (P0 money and statutory movement first, P1 after; waves in §14) but leaves its 22 decisions open. 71.7 now points here and nothing is listed twice.
- **An index, not a copy.** Each item names the BRD section and the FR / UAT ids it must pass; the full acceptance wording stays in the PDF. Build order = its waves: the Wave 1 shared spine before any new economic command.
- **Overlaps point elsewhere.** Stage 73 owns the per-line refund allocation (73.1.1), split tender and refund to the original tender (73.1.6), transfer batch / discrepancy (73.1.7), RFQ / quote / award → PO (73.2.1-73.2.3), the GRN rejected-quantity policy (73.2.4, D05), period reopen (73.1.10), GL actor (73.1.9), report control totals (73.2.19), till cash movements (73.2.7) and the one task inbox (73.3.3). Stage 72 owns item classification (72.1), Consumption Entry (72.6) and the stored-value ledger (72.8) that store credit reuses. E-invoice / e-way credentials stay 20.30 / 20.31 / 26.2.3 / 26.2.4.
- **Every gap was re-checked against source on 2026-10-17**: two read-only sweeps, with file:line evidence in each item. The two claims that change the shared spine were read directly (`inventory.go:353-397`, `numbering.go:118-133`). The BRD itself says its gaps are "not an independently executed code audit"; 74.0.2 lists what it does not know.
- **The first principle applies to the BRD's §8 model.** Its tables and routes are "illustrative; reuse existing routes where possible". Reuse map:
  - number series → Stage 60 `GenerateSequence`
  - approvals → `approval_rules` value slabs + the existing self-approval block
  - reasons → the `ReasonCode` master
  - idempotency → `command_idempotency` / `ClaimCommand`
  - store credit → 72.8's stored-value ledger
  - registers → `ReportDefinition`
  - provider calls → `async_jobs` + `channel_credentials` + the courier adapter pattern
  - prints → Stage 63's print endpoint and templates
  - errors → `writeAPIError` (the BRD's 400 / 403 / 409 / 422)
  
  No second stock ledger, invoice ledger, approval engine or report framework.
- **Tax sign-off sits outside code.** Per the BRD, tax positions need Account Head / qualified tax professional sign-off before production (DEC-02, 03, 10, 14-16, 20). A module can be built and verified on a test client. It is not switched on for a real client until that sign-off exists. Each module ships behind a client setting, default off (NFR-15: old clients unchanged).
- **Acceptance bar = the BRD's §1.2 and its §10 execution rule.** A permissioned user can configure, create, approve, post, partly fulfil, reverse, search, print / reprint and report the transaction, and stock, custody, money, GL and compliance reconcile from the same saved ids. The evidence for each case:
  - the UI action and a readback
  - stock / GL reconciliation
  - the print
  - a denied-role test
  - a replay test

  Each module also gets its guide sections and a `docs/guides/UAT_CHECKLIST.md` section when it ships.

**Pending-item update 2026-10-18.** Nothing in Stage 74 is built yet. Since it was written, another session built most of Stage 73 (uncommitted), and Stages 75-77 took over parts of this stage. Items below carry an *2026-10-18* note where that changes them. In short:

### 74.0 Wave 0 — verify and decide

- [ ] **74.0.1 Decisions DEC-01-DEC-22** **[needs design decision: user, with Finance / Tax sign-off where the BRD names them]**. BRD §13: *"Product and Finance must record signed decisions; Tax/CA owns statutory treatment. Do not let developers silently choose legal rules or GL accounts."* The BRD's default is in brackets, then what the decision blocks:
  - **DEC-01 Challan purpose taxonomy:** which purposes keep stock in custody and which consume it [purpose-led config: returnable / job work = custody; non-returnable sample = expense] → 74.3.1.
  - **DEC-02 Rule 55 print format and numbering** [≤16 characters where the law / provider applies; three copy labels] → 74.1.2, 74.3.1, 74.3.4.
  - **DEC-03 Customer advance tax** (goods vs services; receipt / refund voucher content) [effective-dated policy by supply type / GSTIN; no blanket "no GST"] → 74.2.1.
  - **DEC-04 Using an advance across GSTINs / entities** [no; explicit intercompany accounting only] → 74.2.1.
  - **DEC-05 Advance refund tender and expiry** [original tender; no expiry without an approved legal policy] → 74.2.1.
  - **DEC-06 Material valuation classes and backflush** [four classes: stocked valuated, stocked non-valuated, non-stock, backflush; never expensed twice]. Stage 72.1's dimensions (stock tracking × valuation × consumption) already answer most of it, so confirm rather than re-decide → 74.2.4.
  - **DEC-07 MI / MR cost-centre and account mapping** [posting profile by entity / material group / department] → 74.1.3, 74.2.4.
  - **DEC-08 Adjustment gain / loss accounts and approval** [reason-based posting; independent approval above risk thresholds] → 74.2.3.
  - **DEC-09 Job-work weight / yield / loss tolerance and accepted valuation** [per process / material / weight type; cost allocation reviewed] → 74.3.3.
  - **DEC-10 Job-work statutory due dates, ITC-04, e-way exceptions** [effective legal rules; qualified tax review once a year] → 74.3.3, 74.4.
  - **DEC-11 Sales-return refund method priority** [original tender where possible; store credit by choice under policy]. Same family as Stage 73 D02 → 74.2.2. *2026-10-18:* D02 is decided (the points share goes back as points) and 73.1.6 now refunds each tender back by default. What remains is when store credit may replace the original tender.
  - **DEC-12 Store credit expiry / transfer / cash conversion** [none by default; explicit policy]. Same question as Stage 73 D08 / 72.D(g) → 74.2.2. *2026-10-18:* still open in both places.
  - **DEC-13 Customer identity for credit and advances** [stable customer id; a mobile change is logged; anonymous use only under control] → 74.2.1, 74.2.2.
  - **DEC-14 IRP direct API or GSP** [an adapter that can switch; sandbox pilot first; credentials per entity] → 74.4; the credentials themselves are 20.30 / 26.2.3.
  - **DEC-15 IRN gating when the provider is offline** [fail safe for an eligible document; approved contingency, reconcile later] → 74.4.
  - **DEC-16 E-way exceptions** (precious metal, mixed consignments) [HSN / notification version by goods / route; no blind "silver means exempt" rule] → 74.4.
  - **DEC-17 Reprinting originals after a template change** [render from the frozen statutory payload; version policy documented] → 74.1.4, 74.3.4.
  - **DEC-18 Reason-code stewardship and thresholds** [a steward per category; imported unmapped reasons flagged] → 74.1.1.
  - **DEC-19 When PO / GRN fields become mandatory; multi-PO receipt** [mandatory at approve / dispatch / post, never on a draft] → 74.3.2. *2026-10-18:* the rejected-quantity half is decided (D05, built in 73.2.4).
  - **DEC-20 Mandatory fields on legal invoice / receipt profiles** [tax validation blocks an invalid issue; an offline printer fallback may print afterwards] → 74.3.4.
  - **DEC-21 Migrating history, or switching on for new documents only** [keep historic cash refunds and notes as they are; no synthetic reversal without evidence] → every module; 74.5.5.
  - **DEC-22 Pilot sign-off and severity rules** [Account Head + Tax + Warehouse + Store Ops + Product + Engineering; CEO / steering decides] → 74.5.7.
- **74.0.2 Re-verification against source (2026-10-17): what the BRD does not know.** Use this alongside each item.
  - **Already there, to reuse:**
    - Legal Entity carries GSTIN and state, Locations link to it, and the GL carries `entity` (`migrations_stage17h_location_masters.sql:18-23`, `finance.go:144`).
    - Department and Cost Centre masters exist (`:10-11, 25-30`). A GL posting accepts `PostingOptions{CostCenter, Department, Entity, Project}` (`finance.go:46, 138-149`), but only journal vouchers and expenses pass it.
    - Approval rules use value slabs per doctype, and self-approval is refused under a row lock (`approval.go:26-27, 138-147, 324-339`).
    - Editing an approved PO resets it to pending and needs an `amendment_reason` (`approval.go:523`, `transactional_validation.go:645-669`).
    - `command_idempotency` gives payload-digest and lease semantics (`command_idempotency.go:98, 197`).
    - The GRN posts Dr 1200 / Cr 2100 GRN Suspense, which is the BRD's GRNI (`costing.go:237-252`). The vendor invoice three-way match holds a mismatch (`vendor_invoice.go:103, 212, 295`).
    - Multi-PO GRN keeps the PO on each line (62.1).
    - Vendor invoices are unique per vendor + number + FY (`migrations_stage17g_vendor_invoice.sql:21-27`).
    - `print_job_log` is written by QZ printing (`migrations_stage31_1_qz_print.sql:41-53`).
    - `async_jobs` has backoff, dead letter and replay (`jobrunner.go:73-397`).
    - `channel_credentials` are AES-GCM encrypted with a key ring, and the courier adapter interface can serve as the model (`courier.go:22, 86`).
    - The stored-value profile already offers instrument type "Store credit" with its own liability account (72.8, uncommitted).
  - **Worse than the BRD assumes:**
    - (a) **The stock ledger row is written after the commit, best effort.** `WriteStockLedgerLines` (`inventory.go:353-397`) runs in its own transaction once availability has committed, and a failure is only a WARN in the system log (`:394-395`). This is deliberate, because a replay rewrites it. But it breaks the BRD's one-transaction invariant (§2.2, §8.1 step 4) → 74.1.5.
    - (b) **Cycle count posts no GL.** An approved variance moves quantity only, clamped with `GREATEST(0, …)`. There is no inventory gain or shrinkage account → 74.2.3. *2026-10-18:* 73.1.8 made the post one transaction under the line's row lock, and Approved-only. It still posts no GL, and it still writes its ledger row after the commit (`PostCycleCountAdjustment`, `wms.go:697`).
    - (c) **Stock postings never check a closed period.** `rejectIfCurrentPeriodClosed` runs only inside `PostDoubleEntryTx` and payroll (`accounting_periods.go:256`, `finance.go:182`) → 74.1.5.
    - (d) **Three stock writers bypass the choke point:** transfers, the cycle-count post and WMS bin writes (`transfer_orders.go:168, 298`, `wms.go:697`). A transfer shortfall used to be received nowhere. *2026-10-18:* 73.1.7's lifecycle (`transfer_lifecycle.go`) now tracks damaged / lost / excess / returned per line, lot and serial, each with a *Transfer Discrepancy* reason code. Lost and damaged units still post no GL, and the old single-call receive keeps its free-text reason (TRN-0259) → 74.1.1, 74.2.3.
    - (e) **There are no attachments.** "Attachment" is not a supported field type (`doctype.go:94`); only PIM product media can be uploaded (`pim_media.go:275`) → 74.1.6.
    - (f) **GL accounts are hard-coded** (`finance.go:424-581`, `returns.go:867`, `notes.go:78-114`):
      - POS: 1100 / 1101 / 1102 / 4100 / 5100
      - GRN: 1200 / 2100
      - refund: 4100 / 1100. *2026-10-18:* since 73.1.6 a refund goes back to each tender's clearing account, but a "Store Credit" choice falls through to cash 1100 (`paymentModeClearingAccount` default, `finance.go:444`).
      - credit note: 4150 / 1100

      Only 72.1's item expense account and 72.8's liability account are configurable → 74.1.3. Stage 73 has since added more fixed accounts: 5290 till petty cash (73.2.7) and 2630 loyalty liability (73.2.12).
    - (g) **A POS sales return creates no GST credit note.** It prints as "Sales Return", numbered with the `RR…` document id rather than a series (`print_documents.go:641-643`, `returns_atomic.go:199`). The separate `CreditNote` posts Dr 4150 / Cr 1100 with no tax split (`notes.go:78-114`) → 74.2.2.
    - (h) **Reports have no store / location scope.** They are limited only by module gating, a row cap and sensitive-column masking; `scope_policy.go` is not applied. They export CSV only, and XLSX exists for the vendor PO alone (`report_export.go:38`, `xlsx_writer.go:198`). The BRD wants permission-scoped registers with CSV / XLSX → 74.5.1. *2026-10-18:* the scope half is now 75.3.3, to be done before any new report.
    - (i) **Transaction prints record nothing on the server.** The print data endpoint only returns data, and "printed by / at" is filled in by the browser from its own clock (`trade-docs.js:409-421`). Store, party and GSTIN are read live from today's masters (`print_documents.go:132-177`) and PO lines are re-priced at print time (`:411`), so a reprint after a master change shows different particulars → 74.1.4.
    - (j) **POS session close counts only cash.** Expected cash = opening + gross Cash / blank-mode paid carts, not net of refunds, gift cards or loyalty, with no per-tender totals (`pos_session.go:104-141, 265-324`). An advance or a store-credit movement has no line to land on → 74.2.1, 73.2.7. *2026-10-18: fixed by 73.2.7* (`SessionCashPosition`: float + cash tendered − cash refunds ± paid in / paid out / safe drops). A per-tender close is 77.5. Advances and store credit still need their own lines in it.
    - (k) **Number series gaps:**
      - There is no total-length (16-character) guard; only prefix / suffix ≤20 and padding 1-12 are checked (`number_series.go:53, 168`).
      - The first allocation of a new FY / store bucket inserts without `ON CONFLICT` (`numbering.go:125`), so of two concurrent first saves one fails on the primary key. The number is not duplicated, but the save fails → 74.1.2. *2026-10-18:* the length cap is now 76.7 (a length count against the country cap). The race is unchanged.
  - **As the BRD says, nothing exists for:**
    - Delivery challan. Only Transfer Out prints as a challan, and only for the same GSTIN (`print_documents.go:483-486`).
    - Customer advance. There is no liability account; 2600 is deferred revenue.
    - Internal request / issue / return. There is only production issue, which posts no GL (`manufacturing.go:91-179`), plus 72.6's Consumption Entry.
    - Standalone inventory adjustment.
    - Job work beyond `SubcontractOrder`, which has one sent SKU, one received SKU, one-shot send / receive and no GL (`manufacturing_scheduling.go:195-237`).
    - Store credit. A refund is always Dr 4100 / Cr 1100, and `refund_method` is only a label (`returns.go:867-876`).
    - IRN / e-way bill. There are only unused error-catalog strings.
    - Copy labels, a company logo setting, template versions, and store / channel template selection.
    - PO payment / delivery terms, expected date, quote reference and freight.
    - GRN vendor challan, vehicle, LR, transporter and packages.
    - Customer GSTIN / place of supply on the Sales Invoice, which also has no entry screen (`view-finance.js:946-949`).

### 74.1 Wave 1 — shared spine (before any new economic command)

- [ ] **74.1.1 Reason codes: one governed catalogue, required on every exception** (BRD 4.9, FR-RSN-01..05, UAT-RSN-01..05, DEC-18; was 71.7(i)).
  - **Exists:** the `ReasonCode` master (code, description, category, Active / Inactive; `migrations_stage26_12_foundation.sql:25-28`; no effective dates, no seed outside tests). `requireActiveReasonCode` (`orders.go:721-745`) already guards order hold / cancel, return rejection, short pick, cycle-count variance, settlement, NCR and warehouse-task exceptions. *2026-10-18:* Stage 73 added three more users of the master: transfer discrepancies (73.1.7), PO short-close (73.2.4) and till cash movements (73.2.7).
  - **Add (additive):** the doctype / action contexts each code may be used in; effective from / to; severity → approval routing (the existing engine); "note / evidence required" (74.1.6); an optional loss-class hint for 74.1.3; a "when to use" line; a starter seed per context.
  - **Make it mandatory where free text is taken today:**
    - GRN `rejection_reason` / `damage_reason` (`transactional_validation.go:125-127`, `view-procurement.js:1674, 1685`)
    - Purchase Return `reason` (`purchase_return.go:366`)
    - the old single-call transfer receive (`transfer_orders.go:272-295`); 73.1.7's lifecycle already uses the *Transfer Discrepancy* category
    - POS return `reason` (`returns_atomic.go:78, 93`)
    - POS price override (`pos_quote.go:549, 585`)
    - sticker and document reprint
  - **Validation:** server-side at submit and again at post, so a code disabled between draft and post is refused. The event stores the code id plus a snapshot of label and version, so a renamed or retired code still reads as it was.
  - **History:** historic free text stays as written and is flagged "unmapped" (DEC-21).
  - **Screen and reports:** Setup → Reason Codes, with category tabs. R-TX-12 GRN rejection / purchase return by reason; R-TX-13 transfer shortage by reason.
- [ ] **74.1.2 Number series for the new documents, with a legal-length guard** (BRD §2.2, §3.1, DEC-02). Built on Stage 60's `GenerateSequence`, with no second counter. *2026-10-18: mostly Stage 76 now:*
  - the per-store / per-transaction numbering module, with a length count against the country cap (76.7-76.8)
  - the customer credit note and the other tax documents (76.9)
  - Sales Return and the other `NewDocID` documents (76.11)
  - rows for Customer Advance, MI, MR, Damage and Delivery Challan (76.12)

  What stays here: add IA, JO, JR, CV and AVR to 76.12's list, plus the race fix and the typed fields below.
  - **New series:** DC, CA, AVR (advance refund voucher), MI, MR, IA, JO, JR, CV (store credit voucher), and a sales-return GST credit-note series (74.2.2).
  - **Legal-length guard:** a per-series "legal document" flag that refuses, at preview and save in `number_series.go`, any format whose longest possible number exceeds 16 characters.
  - **Race fix:** the first-allocation race (`numbering.go:125`) becomes `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` followed by a re-select under the lock.
  - **Typed fields:** keep the logical id apart from the displayed number. Source invoice no., return no., GST credit-note no., IRN and EWB no. each get their own typed field (§3.1).
- [ ] **74.1.3 Posting profiles: configurable accounts per event** (BRD §3, §5, DEC-07, DEC-08). Accounts are hard-coded today (74.0.2 f).
  - **One `PostingProfile` master.** It maps legal entity + event + optional valuation class / reason / cost centre to a debit and a credit account, with effective dates and an approved version. Events:
    - advance collected / applied / refunded
    - store credit issued / redeemed / expired
    - MI / MR
    - IA gain / loss / damage / revaluation
    - job-work charge / loss
    - non-returnable challan issue
  - **Resolution:** at the posting choke point, with today's constants as the shipped default, so existing postings do not change (DEC-21).
  - **Seed the new accounts additively:** customer advance liability, store credit liability, inventory gain, shrinkage / damage expense, operating supplies expense, job-work conversion cost / WIP.
  - **Control:** editing a profile is maker-checker.
- [ ] **74.1.4 Issued-output snapshot and print / reprint log** (BRD 4.8, FR-PRT-01..06, UAT-PRT-01..05, DEC-17; was 71.7(h)).
  - **Log:** extend `print_job_log` rather than adding a table. New columns:
    - source doctype / id / number / revision
    - template id + version and copy label
    - original / reprint flag, reason code (74.1.1) and approver
    - payload checksum and requested / confirmed count
    - outcome: Render Requested → Rendered → Submitted → Printed / Failed / Unknown
  - **Snapshot:** `BuildPrintDocument` (`print_documents.go:383`) freezes the payload when the document is first issued: party, store, GSTIN, prices, tax. Later prints render from that snapshot and carry a reprint mark. "Printed by / at" comes from the server.
  - **Outcome truth:** a browser `window.print()` can only report "sent to browser", never "Printed". QZ's Submitted stays Submitted.
  - **Reprint control:** a reprint needs a permission and a reason, plus manager approval above a configured count or for high-risk documents. A three-copy challan set counts as copies, not reprints.
  - **Report:** R-TX-16 print / reprint audit, with repeat-count anomalies.
  - **Stickers:** keep `sticker_print_log`, with the reason moved to a code.
- [ ] **74.1.5 Shared command spine for every new economic document** (BRD §2.1-2.2, §8.1, §8.3, NFR-01/02/06). One helper that 74.2-74.3 all use, built on what exists:
  - `ClaimCommand` idempotency: a replay returns the same result; reusing a key with a different payload → 409.
  - The `expected_version` revision token (stale → 409).
  - Source rows locked `FOR UPDATE`, server-recomputed totals, and entity / location / role scope.
  - Typed source ↔ child line links, drillable both ways (§9.1).
  - The poster on every GL row: already done by 73.1.9 (`posting_actor.go` stamps it inside `PostDoubleEntryTx`).

  It also needs two things that do not exist yet:
  1. A `WriteStockLedgerLinesTx` variant, so the stock ledger, availability, GL, document event and outbox commit in one transaction. Today's post-commit write (74.0.2 a) stays for existing callers until each is moved deliberately.
  2. A closed-period check on stock postings (74.0.2 c).

  **Rules:**
  - Statuses are kept as separate fields: Draft / Approved / Posted / Printed / Dispatched / IRN / EWB / Reconciled are never collapsed into one (§2.1).
  - A posted document cannot be deleted through the generic doc API; a reversal is a linked reversing document.
  - Errors are 400 / 403 / 409 / 422 through `writeAPIError`.
- [ ] **74.1.6 Evidence attachments on documents** (BRD 4.4 step 1, 4.9, UAT-RSN-03 "no post without proof"). No generic attachment exists (74.0.2 e).
  - **Mechanism:** one shared upload linked to (doctype, id, line), with type and size limits. *2026-10-18:* stored through 75.4.1's `MediaStore` (planned, not built), so attachments move to object storage with everything else.
  - **Access:** role-scoped download (49.3.1 already lists attachments in its authorisation contract); never publicly readable (NFR-12).
  - **Used by:** adjustments, reason codes that require evidence, transfer shortages and GRN rejections.

### 74.2 Wave 2 — money first (P0)

- [ ] **74.2.1 Customer Advance Receipt** (BRD 4.2, FR-ADV-01..08, UAT-ADV-01..07, E2E-01, DEC-03/04/05/13; was 71.7(b)). Nothing exists today.
  - **Document `CustomerAdvance`** (series CA).
    - Fields: customer (stable id; a mobile change is logged), entity / store / channel, a reference (custom order, image, hold request, future SKU, expected date), tender legs + payment reference, POS session.
    - Statuses: Draft → Payment Pending → Collected (Open) → Partially Applied → Fully Applied / Refunded / Reversed. It can be cancelled only while unpaid.
  - **Collection:** Dr cash / bank clearing per tender, Cr Customer Advance Liability (74.1.3). Tax per DEC-03, never hard-coded. Prints a receipt voucher.
  - **Gateway timeout after capture** (UAT-ADV-07): collection is a 74.1.5 command, so the payment reference is reconciled before any retry.
  - **Applying it at the till or on a Sales Invoice:**
    - The bill keeps its own SI number.
    - Allocations are append-only rows under a row lock, so two tills cannot spend the same 250 (UAT-ADV-04).
    - Same customer and entity only (DEC-04).
    - Posts Dr Advance Liability / Cr AR / clearing, never revenue.
    - *2026-10-18:* plugs into 73.1.6's split tender (built, `pos_tenders.go`) as its own tender, and into 77.5's tender master once that exists. The CA series row comes from 76.12.
  - **Refund:** needs an approved reason, an open period and the original tender (DEC-05). Prints a refund voucher (AVR), posts Dr liability / Cr cash / bank, and can happen once. Returning a bill partly settled by an advance follows 73.1.1's tender allocation.
  - **POS close:** advances received and refunded feed 73.2.7's `SessionCashPosition` (built) as their own movement. The per-tender close is 77.5.
  - **Screens:**
    - POS → Take Advance, beside billing.
    - Checkout shows Available Advance and Applied / Remaining.
    - Advance Register with Open / Expiring / Fully Applied / Refunded.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-02 outstanding + ageing, tied to the GL liability; R-TX-03 settlement / tender.
  - **Identity (§11.1):** opening + receipts − allocations − refunds − reversals = closing liability, per entity and customer.
- [ ] **74.2.2 Store credit and the refund-method choice** (BRD 4.6, §5.1, FR-SC-01..08, UAT-SC-01..06, E2E-04, DEC-11/12/13; was 71.7(f)). When this was written, every refund was Dr 4100 / Cr 1100 cash, whatever `refund_method` said (`returns.go:867-876`). *2026-10-18:* since 73.1.6 a refund goes back to each original tender's clearing account. Choosing "Store Credit" still credits cash 1100 (`finance.go:444` default), so store credit still does not exist.
  - **Dependencies 73.1.1 and 73.1.6 are built** (2026-10-17, `sale_allocation.go`, `pos_tenders.go`). This can start once DEC-11 / DEC-12 are answered.
  - **Refund method per return**, from what the original tenders allow (DEC-11): original cash / card / UPI / bank, store credit, exchange, or a mix. Never unconditional cash (FR-SC-01). A bill paid with store credit gets credit back, not cash (FR-SC-06).
  - **Store credit lives on 72.8's stored-value ledger**, not a second ledger. A "Store Credit" profile already exists as an instrument type (`migrations_stage72_8_stored_value.sql:107`); give it its own liability account (74.1.3). Gaps to close:
    - An issue path funded by a refund (Dr refund payable / clearing, Cr Store Credit Liability). Today issue always debits payment clearing (`stored_value.go:503-505`) and needs a pre-registered card number (`:460`).
    - A customer-bound instrument with a masked token instead of a physical card.
  - **Redemption** already runs inside checkout, with a row lock and idempotency key (`stored_value.go:661-696`). Add:
    - a customer / entity / store check (`customer_id` is never checked at redeem today)
    - OTP or manager approval above a threshold
    - a rate limit
  - **Expiry / forfeiture** only through a logged, approved policy run (DEC-12), never a silent cron.
  - **GST credit note, kept separate from the voucher** (74.0.2 g). An eligible return gets a sales-return tax reversal and its own credit-note number from a series (76.9's CRN), with the original invoice reference. The credit voucher is the liability instrument, not a tax document (FR-SC-02). Add the missing tax split to `PostCreditNote` (`notes.go:78-114`) at the same time.
  - **Points on a returned sale:** 73.1.2 / 73.1.3 (built).
  - **Screens:** Returns → Refund Method selector with the split. The POS tender drawer shows Store Credit beside cash / card / UPI / advance / gift card / points, plus a receipt line.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-07 store credit liability (= GL, by entity); R-TX-08 refund methods reconciliation.
  - **Identity:** opening + issued / restored − redeemed − refunded / expired = outstanding.
- [ ] **74.2.3 Standalone Inventory Adjustment** (BRD 4.4, FR-ADJ-01..08, UAT-ADJ-01..06, E2E-05, DEC-08; was 71.7(d)). Today adjustments exist only as cycle-count variances, which post no GL (74.0.2 b).
  - **Document `InventoryAdjustment`** (series IA).
    - Classes: gain, loss, damage write-off, found stock, cost revaluation (value only).
    - Lines: SKU / lot / serial / bin, signed delta, before / after, unit cost, value, stock owner.
    - Each line carries a reason (74.1.1) and evidence (74.1.6).
  - **Preview:** shows the locked before qty, delta, after qty, cost and GL effect. The post recomputes and refuses a stale snapshot (409, UAT-ADJ-04).
  - **Approval:** maker-checker through `approval_rules` value slabs; the self-approval block already exists (`approval.go:337-339`).
  - **Posting** (through 74.1.5), quantity and value in one transaction:
    - gain: Dr 1200 / Cr Inventory Gain
    - loss: Dr Shrinkage / Damage / Cr 1200 (accounts via 74.1.3)
    - A damaged unit that is still owned moves to a QC / non-sellable condition instead of being written off.
    - A serial cannot be duplicated or written off twice.
  - **Reversal, never deletion:** a linked reversing IA in an open period.
  - **Cycle count gets the same posting:** an approved count variance posts gain / shrinkage too, which closes 74.0.2 b. 73.1.8's one-transaction post is built; add the GL leg and move its ledger write inside the transaction. Transfer lost / damaged units (73.1.7) take the same loss posting.
  - **Screen:** Stock → Adjust Stock, separate from Count.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-05 register, a reason / location loss dashboard, and stock ledger ↔ GL. 73.2.19's inventory-valuation control total against 1200 is built (`control_totals.go`); reuse it. Flag repeated positive adjustments by one user.
- [ ] **74.2.4 Internal Material Issue and Material Receipt** (BRD 4.3, FR-MAT-01..07, UAT-MAT-01..05, DEC-06/07; was 71.7(c)). Only production issue (no GL) and 72.6's Consumption Entry exist; in the latter the poster is also the approver.
  - **Flow:** request → approve → issue (MI) → return (MR) against the issue line. Each carries department, employee, cost centre, project and purpose; the `Department` / `CostCenter` masters already exist.
  - **Item behaviour from 72.1's dimensions:**
    - valuated: stock out, Dr expense (posting profile, with `PostingOptions{CostCenter, Department}` on the GL row) / Cr 1200
    - non-valuated: quantity only
    - non-stock: refused, so no phantom on-hand
  - **Approval:** the requester cannot approve their own request; over-limit issues go through the approval slabs.
  - **MR rules:** return ≤ issued − already returned, per line. Condition is captured; damaged goods go to QC, not to sellable stock. Posts Dr 1200 / Cr the original expense.
  - **One source per consumption** (UAT-MAT-05):
    - A manual MI of an item whose consumption is Auto backflush (`item_policy.go:105`) is refused.
    - 72.6's Consumption Entry gets the same guard; today it checks neither the method nor a prior POSConsumption.
    - 72.6 stays as the count-based periodic mode beside the request-based MI / MR.
  - **Cross-entity issue:** refused; use a transfer.
  - **Screens:** My Requests / Ready to Issue / Return Remaining.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-04 consumption by cost centre, the MI / MR register, and packaging and GWP cost (with 72.13).

### 74.3 Wave 3 — movement and vendor

- [ ] **74.3.1 Delivery Challan** (BRD 4.1, FR-DC-01..07, UAT-DC-01..06, E2E-03/06, DEC-01/02; was 71.7(a)). Today only a same-GSTIN Transfer Out prints as a challan.
  - **Document `DeliveryChallan`** (series DC, ≤16 characters).
    - Header: purpose (from a purpose master per DEC-01: job work, sample, repair, exhibition, returnable / non-returnable, other); from location → entity / GSTIN; consignee (vendor / customer / job worker / free party with GSTIN, address, state, place of supply).
    - Lines: item / design / combination, HSN, qty / UOM, lot / serial, gross / net weight, declared value, tax if any, expected return, condition.
    - Transport: mode, LR / AWB, vehicle, transporter id, packages, dispatch date; plus a reference document.
    - Statuses: Draft → Submitted → Approved → Issued → Dispatched → Partially / Fully Returned → Closed. It can be cancelled only before dispatch, which releases the reservation.
  - **External custody.** None exists today: Location types are only Store / Warehouse / HO (`migrations_stage17h_location_masters.sql:17`).
    - Owned returnable goods leave available stock at dispatch and sit in a tracked custody bucket per counterparty. That is not a sale: no COGS and no change in total inventory value.
    - The lightest way to build it: an External / Job-worker location per counterparty, reusing stock and the transfer in-transit pattern.
    - Non-returnable goods are issued against the posting profile's expense.
  - **Returns:** per line, partial and multiple; accepted / rejected / damaged / lost. *2026-10-18:* reuse 73.1.7's transfer lifecycle (balance per line / lot / serial, `receipt_key` replay, return-to-source) rather than building a new one.
    - The open balance stays until an approved waiver / write-off / conversion, never an auto-close (10 issued, 4 returned, 1 damaged → 6 open).
    - A returned serial must match an issued one.
  - **E-way decision** is stored on the challan with its rule evidence; generating the bill is 74.4.
  - **Print:** three copies (Original for Consignee / Duplicate for Transporter / Triplicate for Consignor) logged as one set (74.1.4, 74.3.4), with the Rule 55 fields in the §6.1 layout.
  - **Screen:** Stock → Delivery Challans, with tabs Open Returns / Due Soon / Exceptions / EWB Status, and Receive Return on each line.
  - **Report:** R-TX-01 register + outstanding returnables by counterparty and due age.
- [ ] **74.3.2 PO commercial terms and GRN logistics** (BRD 4.10, FR-POGRN-01..07, UAT-POGRN-01..06, E2E-02, DEC-19; was 71.7(j) and the 71.6 list).
  - **PO (additive fields):**
    - payment terms as a master with net days, delivery terms / Incoterm, expected delivery date. *2026-10-18:* 73.2.3 added `payment_terms` as free text, filled from the awarded quote.
    - quotation reference: *done for awarded quotes*. 73.2.3 creates the PO with `source_rfq_id` / `source_quote_id` (`migrations_stage73_2_3_award_to_po.sql`). Quote validity on the PO is still to add.
    - freight responsibility and vendor contact. *2026-10-18:* 73.2.3 added `freight_amount` (the quoted amount); who bears the freight is still to add.
    - Bill-to / ship-to stay derived from location / warehouse, with an authorised override.
    - All required at approve, not on a draft (DEC-19).
    - Snapshot on approval. Editing approved terms already re-approves and asks for an `amendment_reason`; also record the before / after in the audit (UAT-POGRN-06).
  - **GRN (additive fields):**
    - vendor challan no. / date, kept separate from the invoice
    - received date / time, LR / AWB, vehicle, transporter, package count
    - per line: ordered / previously received / accepted / rejected / open, with reason codes (74.1.1), and weight / batch where the item type asks for it (Stage 61)
  - **Duplicate vendor invoice no.** (per vendor / entity / FY): detected at the GRN as well. Today the unique index is on VendorInvoice only.
  - **Multi-PO GRN:** add a mixed-currency refusal beside GOODSR-0318's mixed-vendor refusal.
  - **Rejected / short policy: done in 73.2.4 (D05).**
    - Only accepted units fulfil a line; rejected units stay open for replacement.
    - A PO can be short-closed with a reason.
    - One view shows ordered / accepted / rejected / open / invoiced / paid.

    The BRD's fixture (80 delivered, 75 accepted, 5 rejected, 25 not yet accepted) matches it, so UAT-POGRN-03 is a check, not a build.
  - **GRNI:** 2100 already plays the role. Add the R-TX-15 GRNI report: accepted value vs vendor invoice, price difference, AP holds.
  - **Prints:** the PO, GRN and vendor-copy Standard layouts print the new fields.
  - **Screens:** the PO composer groups Commercial Terms / Deliveries / References; the GRN groups Supplier Documents / Transport & Packages / the accepted-rejected grid / QC.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-14 PO expected delivery and terms, GRN discrepancy by vendor, and a vendor document / transport register.
- [ ] **74.3.3 Job Work Outward and Return** (BRD 4.5, FR-JW-01..08, UAT-JW-01..06, E2E-03/14, DEC-09/10; was 71.7(e)). Upgrade `SubcontractOrder` (`migrations_stage26_9_10_scheduling_subcontract.sql:26-36`, `manufacturing_scheduling.go:195-237`) rather than adding a parallel doctype.
  - **Outward:** many source lines (lot / serial, qty, gross / net / fine weight, purity, value), dispatched through Delivery Challans (74.3.1) into the job worker's custody.
  - **Returns:** many partial returns against the original lines.
    - Each records accepted / rejected / scrap / loss and QC.
    - An output SKU mapping covers transformation with yield, and genealogy is kept.
    - Rejected pieces never enter a sellable location.
  - **Balance per source line**, in qty and in each weight: outward − accepted returned − approved scrap / loss − transfers to the next processor = open.
    - Tolerance per DEC-09; above it, an independent approval.
  - **Processing charge:** from the vendor bill, Dr WIP / conversion cost, Cr vendor, allocated to the returned items. Loss goes to its profile account.
  - **Overdue / statutory due dates** (DEC-10, ITC-04 data where applicable): flagged, with reminders.
  - **Weights:** today they exist only as jewellery-profile Item fields (`public/profiles/jewelry.json:49-55`). Put them on the job-work lines.
  - **Migration:** existing one-SKU orders become one-line orders with balances unchanged (E2E-14).
  - **Screen:** a Job Work workbench (Orders / Send / Receive Partial / QC / Charges / Exceptions), showing per line Outward | Returned | Approved Loss | Remaining in qty and weight.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-06 outstanding + weight ageing, and cost / yield / wastage.
- [ ] **74.3.4 Print profiles: legal identity, copy labels, receipt and invoice formats** (BRD 4.11, FR-PF-01..08, UAT-PF-01..06, DEC-17/20; was 71.7(k)).
  - **Legal identity from the Legal Entity, not the template.** Add registered address, trade name, contact, bank / terms and an approved logo to `LegalEntity`. *2026-10-18:* address lines and the state code come from 76.2 / 76.3 (76.3 in progress). Trade name, contact, bank / terms and the logo stay here.
    - Today it holds only code / name / GSTIN / state. The brand comes from `print.brand_name`, and a logo exists only as an image inside a template.
    - A store override can change the display, never the seller GSTIN.
  - **Profile selection:** add store, channel and paper / device to the `PrintTemplate` mapping beside today's vendor / family / category weights (`print_templates.go:15-18`).
    - Deterministic precedence: document-specific → site → entity + channel → entity default → platform fallback.
    - Every save creates a template version, and 74.1.4 logs which version printed. Old versions can be restored; the life-cycle is preview → approve → active.
  - **Copy labels per document** (BRD §6 table); one print run = one set.
  - **POS receipt:**
    - Profile-driven width. 58 / 80 mm already works via the printer's `label_width_mm` (`qz_payload.go:465-471`), but the browser fallback is a fixed 280 px (`styles.css:2062`).
    - Add the tax breakup and HSN where required, every tender (advance / store credit / gift card / points), change due and a return-policy footer.
    - Long numbers wrap without clipping (UAT-PF-03).
  - **Sales Invoice:**
    - Add customer GSTIN and place of supply fields. Today the IGST / CGST-SGST split is derived from the order ship-to (`order_invoice.go:115-124`). *2026-10-18:* the POS B2B tax invoice (buyer GSTIN, place of supply, HSN-wise summary) is 77.15; use one set of fields for both.
    - Show the IRN / signed QR block only when 74.4 marks the document eligible.
    - Build a real entry screen.
  - **Distinct formats** for tax invoice, credit / debit note, receipt voucher, refund voucher and challan.
  - **A missing entity GSTIN** blocks issuing a tax document with an actionable message (UAT-PF-05).
  - **Screen:** Settings → Printing (Profiles / Company Branding / Templates / Test Print / Printers / History).
  - **Reports:** template coverage per transaction, failed prints and fallbacks, missing regulatory data.

### 74.4 Wave 4 — e-invoice (IRN / signed QR) and e-way bill (BRD 4.7, FR-GST-01..10, UAT-GST-01..10, E2E-06/12, DEC-14/15/16; was 71.7(g))

**2026-10-17: the IRN half of 74.4.1–74.4.5 and 74.4.7 is built under Stage 75.2** (IRIS IRP-6 plan, `docs/product/einvoice-iris-irp-plan-2026-10-17.md`); tick those here when 75.2 closes. The e-way bill (74.4.6) stays here.

Live credentials stay **20.30 / 20.31 / 26.2.3 / 26.2.4** (blocked on the user), and the flow itself is 26.6.9. The BRD adds work that can be built and tested before any credentials, against a fake / sandbox adapter. **Exit gate:** sandbox SIT, then qualified Tax / CA sign-off, then a pilot (§14).

- [ ] **74.4.1 Eligibility engine** (needs no provider).
  - **IRN:** per entity / GSTIN, effective-dated rules covering the AATO threshold, notified classes, B2B / export / excluded entity, and INV / CRN / DBN. A B2C sale never calls the IRP.
  - **EWB:** evaluated independently for Sales Invoice, Delivery Challan, Transfer and Job Work, on consignment value, goods / HSN exceptions (DEC-16) and inter-State job work below the ordinary threshold.
  - **Evidence:** every decision stores its rule version.
- [ ] **74.4.2 Pre-submission validation.**
  - Checks: identity, HSN, state codes, totals = lines, the 16-character number, and the original reference on a note.
  - A stable payload hash.
  - Actionable errors.
- [ ] **74.4.3 Provider adapter on the existing pattern.**
  - `async_jobs` for backoff, dead letter and replay.
  - `channel_credentials` per entity, with sandbox / live isolation.
  - An interface like `CourierAdapter`, so direct IRP and a GSP can be swapped (DEC-14).
  - **On a timeout:** query by document identity before any retry; never resubmit blind.
  - **Storage:** raw request / response stored redacted. The signed QR and signed invoice are stored byte-exact and printed unchanged, never self-generated.
  - **Callbacks:** authenticated and scoped (§8.3).
- [ ] **74.4.4 Status models kept apart from the document's own status.**
  - **IRN:** NotRequired / Ready / Submitted / Pending / Generated / Rejected / CancelRequested / Cancelled / UnknownNeedsReconcile.
  - **EWB:** NotRequired / Ready / Submitted / Generated / Failed / Expired / Cancelled / Reconciled.
  - **Provider offline:** DEC-15's contingency. A timeout is never shown as success.
- [ ] **74.4.5 Locks and corrections.**
  - No tax-field edit after a signed QR; issue a corrective note instead.
  - IRN cancel only inside the provider window (24 h), and only by the tax role.
  - The 30-day reporting limit (AATO ≥ ₹10 crore) is an effective-dated warning / block.
- [ ] **74.4.6 E-way bill life-cycle.**
  - Generate (Part A / B).
  - Vehicle update, extension and cancel.
  - Validity monitoring.
- [ ] **74.4.7 GST Compliance workbench and reconciliation.**
  - **IRN queues:** To Submit / Waiting / Rejected / Generated / Cancelled.
  - **E-Way Bills:** Required / Ready / Expiring / Failed.
  - **Reconciliation:** a daily ERP vs IRP / EWB register match, with a suspense queue.
  - **Reports:** R-TX-09 IRN submissions and exceptions; R-TX-10 EWB register.

## Stage 76 — Short codes, store-wise transaction numbering, state dropdowns, address lines, country-correct tax IDs (opened 2026-10-17 from the user's New Vendor screenshot)

**Why it was opened.** On the New Vendor dialog: the code comes out as `Vendor/HQ/2026/000006` (no series confirmed → fallback shape with store and year), State is free text and optional, the address is one line, and GSTIN is checked against India's shape only, with no check digit and no regard to the home country. The inventory behind 76.7-76.11 found worse on the transaction side: the POS bill number is made by the browser (`POS-<store>-<epoch>`, `view-pos.js:951`), sales invoices / credit notes / JVs are 32-character `NewDocID`s, and series numbers use the maker's session store rather than the document's own store. None of those meet India's 16-character rule.

**Decisions (user, 2026-10-17):**
- **D1** Existing long master codes are re-coded to the short style, with every reference updated (user chose "Re-code everything" over keep-old).
- **D2** Transaction numbers are user-controlled **per store, per transaction** (PO, GRN, ST/TO, TI, POS bill, advance, return, MI, MR, damage, ...). A store with no series cannot transact. A new store appears in the module automatically. The final length is capped at the country's legal maximum - India **16** (CGST Rule 46(b); the IRP portal also refuses longer). No published cap was found for the other home countries, so they get a product cap of 20, shown on the screen.
- **D3** State starts blank and must be picked. It fills itself from the GSTIN when there is one, and a State that contradicts the GSTIN is refused.
- **D4** (user: "better practices are ok, but short") A master code is a prefix plus a zero-padded number, with no separator, store or year, never resetting: `V00001`. The prefix and length are asked for **in the master's own New form** the first time.
- **D5** State short code = ISO 3166-2 (`KA`; 2023 codes CG/OD/TS/UK, older ones accepted). It is filled into `state_code` on setups (Legal Entity, Location) and not shown on Vendor.
- **D6** Every address = Address Line 1 (the existing field, relabelled - no data moves) + Address Line 2 + City + State + PIN code.
- **D7** The tax ID follows the home country: India GSTIN with its check digit; 22 other countries with a certain rule; the rest get 5-20 letters/digits.

### 76.A Masters and addresses

- [x] **76.1** Regions table `engines/regions.go` (India: 36 states/UTs + Other Territory; legacy 25/28/99 recognised, not offered). `gst_place_of_supply.go` derives its tables from it. Served with the field formats (`country`, `regions`). *Done 2026-10-17:* `TestResolveRegion` (names, short codes, GST codes, old spellings CT/OR/TG, legacy 25 recognised but not offered); `FieldFormatSpecsFor("IN")` serves 37 regions.
- [x] **76.2** State fields: `applyDocumentRegions` at `validateDocumentWith` (before the mandatory check) - canonical name, fill from GSTIN, mismatch refused, `state_code` companion, Location takes its Legal Entity's state; masters strict, transactions never lose an order. Vendor.state mandatory. Generic form renders every Data `state`/`*_state` field as a dropdown of the home country's states; GSTIN fills it as you type. *Done 2026-10-17:* `engines/document_regions.go` + migration `76_2`; `TestDocumentRegions`; live on :8146 - GSTIN `29…` typed into New Vendor sets State to Karnataka as you type, Maharashtra against it is refused with both names, the inline **+ New** dialog renders State as a dropdown. Fixtures that imported Vendors with no State (`import_master_invariant_test`, `bld046_request_cancellation_test`) now carry one.
- [/] **76.3** Address lines: `address` relabelled Address Line 1, plus `address_line2` (+ City, PIN code where missing) on Vendor, Location and Legal Entity; SalesOrder shipping address the same; OMS manual order form split; prints join the lines. *Partly done 2026-10-17:* migration `76_2` (Line 1 relabelled, Line 2 / City / PIN / State / State Code added; SalesOrder line 2 / city / PIN) and `addressLines` in the PO and party print blocks (`print_documents.go`, `purchase_order.go`). **Left:** the OMS manual-order form split (`view-oms.js`) and the POS receipt (`qz_payload.go`) - both have another session's uncommitted edits.
- [x] **76.4** Tax IDs: `engines/tax_ids.go` - per-country rule, GSTIN check digit (`ValidGSTINChecksum`), ABN checksum; label and hint follow the country; the browser runs the same pattern/check. Test fixtures with made-up GSTINs moved to check-digit-valid ones. *Done 2026-10-17:* `TestGSTINCheckDigit`, `TestTaxIDFormatsPerCountry` (IN/AE/SA/AU/GB/DE/generic). ## Stage 77 — POS billing for a real store: customer capture, high-value rules by country, tenders, salesmen, hold/resume, store returns, scan without Enter, stock locations (opened 2026-10-17 from the user's POS screenshots, during Stage 76)

**Why it was opened.** Four messages from the user while Stage 76 was being built, on the POS screen. The inventory (2026-10-17) found:
- **Customer.** The quick-add asks for name + phone only. Customer has no address, anniversary, gender or PAN, and there is no default customer. The receipt prints the raw customer *code* (`qz_payload.go:542`).
- **Missing.** No high-value rules, no tender master (Cash/Card/UPI hard-coded, `view-pos.js:122`; one `payment_mode` per bill), no salesman, no hold/resume.
- **Returns.** Bill lookup is tenant-wide by exact number. A return at store B of a store-A bill just lands at B.
- **Stock.** Buckets are fixed columns plus a fixed `bin_stock.condition` CHECK. There is no stock-location master, no per-store enablement and no per-transaction from/to defaults.
- **Session close** adds item price × qty for cash bills - discounts and cash refunds are not subtracted.

**User's words (2026-10-17), the scope:**
- Customer name + number (number optional), address, DOB, DOA, gender, PAN. No number → a default customer with a default number, never printed on the customer copy; a real number is printed.
- Bill ≥ ₹50k → ask address; > ₹2 lakh → PAN mandatory - India's rule, and the equivalent for other countries.
- A tender module: which tenders each store bills with, under the user's control; reports pick them up.
- Salesman name/ID before saving - optional per configuration; different per store; always a default salesman; prompt to create one for a store; disable one who left.
- Hold bill / resume bill.
- Sales return: search the store's bills. Centralised return: SR on the original store, TO from the original store, TI at the store where the customer is, into the inward location.
- Stock locations inside a store: Main (sellable), Sold (physically gone), In-Transit (TO done), Inward (after GRN / TI), Purchase Return (warehouse only), Damage. Logic settable; a location master; enable per store; defaults applied to every new store; per transaction a default from/to; a new transaction (e.g. Damage) appears there automatically.
- Scanning without Enter, everywhere (POS, TO, TI, advance, return), typing still possible.
- POS screen: the customer messages take too much room; give the space to the products.

**Rules found (research 2026-10-17, to be held as settings with country defaults, not hard-coded):**
- India, address ≥ ₹50,000 to an unregistered buyer: CGST Rule 46(e) - name, address, delivery address, state + code.
- India, PAN for a sale of goods/services > ₹2,00,000 per transaction: Income-tax Rules 2026 Rule 159 (was 114B). A buyer with no PAN signs Form 97 (was Form 60).
- India, cash of ₹2,00,000 or more from one person per transaction/day is prohibited (s.269ST) - the cash part must be refused, the balance taken by card/UPI.
- Elsewhere, full invoice details above a simplified-invoice limit: UK £250, Germany €250, UAE AED 10,000. No rule found for the others - setting left blank.

- [x] **77.0** Decisions (user, 2026-10-17):
  - **Default customer: one per store** - each store its own walk-in customer and placeholder number (1111111111, 2222222222 …), never printed.
  - **Salesman: a separate Salesman list** (not the Employee master) - per store, default salesman, Active / Left.
  - **Stock locations: a master over today's buckets** - additive; enable per store, defaults for new stores, from/to per transaction, mapped onto the existing bucket columns / bin conditions so no stock data moves.
- [ ] **77.1** Scan without Enter, everywhere: one delegated detector in `app.js` on every scan field (`isScanField`) - a fast keystroke burst is a scanner and submits itself when it stops; human typing behaves as today.
- [ ] **77.2** POS layout: one-line customer status, no stacked messages; the items table gets the height.
- [ ] **77.3** Customer at POS: quick-add with name, number (optional), Address Line 1/2, City, State, PIN, DOB, DOA, gender, PAN; default customer + default number when none is given; the receipt prints the name and a real number, never the default one or the code.
- [ ] **77.4** High-value rules per country (settings above), enforced at checkout on the server and asked for on the till before Complete Sale.

- [ ] **77.6** Salesman per store: default salesman, active/left, ask-before-save off/optional/mandatory per store; stored on the bill; salesman sales report.

(each shows only what applies; no loyalty/coupon/discount where the transaction has none). The session row folds into the header (status chip + Open/Close in a menu), not a row of its own. Builds on Stage 68's frozen screens.
