## Stage 78 — Day limits: PO auto-cancel, purchase return window, and the rest of the set (opened + 78.1-78.2 built 2026-10-17 from the user's message)

User, 2026-10-17: *"i need a days config for po auto cancel. I need all set of days config. po, pr allowed, etc."* Decided with the user the same day: **PR = Purchase Return**; auto-cancel counts **from approval**; a **partly received** PO at the deadline has the **rest short-closed** (consistent with "PO can't be deleted if GRN done"); the rest of the set - *"search the practices followed in ERPs and bring"* - is 78.3, awaiting the user's pick. Existing day settings before this: `procurement.po_edit_window_days`, `procurement.grn_edit_window_days`, `sales.return_window_days` (30).

- [x] **78.1 PO auto-cancel** (`procurement.po_auto_cancel_days`, Int, default 0 = never; `engines/po_auto_cancel.go`). `RunPOAutoCancel` per tenant, `StartPOAutoCancelWorker` hourly in `routes.go` (the dunning-worker shape). Due = Approved, not deleted, approval (latest `approval_log` "Approved"; a PO approved without an approval rule has none and counts from creation) at least N days old. Nothing received (`PurchaseOrderReceipts` empty) → `Cancelled` with `auto_cancelled`, `cancel_reason`, `cancelled_by: system`, in one UPDATE that re-checks no non-cancelled GRN names it (direct or `po_ids`), audit `PURCHASE_ORDER_AUTO_CANCELLED`. Partly received → the existing short-close (split into `ShortClosePurchaseOrder` = reason-code check + `shortClosePurchaseOrder`), reason `AUTO-EXPIRY`, claims for rejected / damaged goods as usual. Fully received (PURCHA-0488) and Draft / Pending Approval are left alone; per-PO failures are logged and the rest continue. PO list (`view-procurement.js`): **Auto-cancelled** stamp under the status (reason on hover); **Edit** and **Send to Vendor** hidden on a Cancelled PO, and `MarkPurchaseOrderSent` refuses one - **PURCHA-0621** (new).
- [x] **78.2 Purchase return window** (`procurement.purchase_return_window_days`, Int, default 0 = no limit). `purchaseReturnable` reads the GRN's age (`CURRENT_DATE - created_at::date`, no Go-side timezone parsing) into `PurchaseReturnable.AgeDays` / `WindowDays`; `resolvePurchaseReturnLines` (save and post both run it) refuses an **Accepted** line past the window - **PURCHA-0620** (new). **Rejected / Damaged stay returnable** (QC-held goods must always be able to go back; a short-close claim can come long after the receipt) - a judgement call, flagged to the user.
- **Verification (2026-10-17):** `go build ./...`, `go vet ./engines`; new `TestProcurementDayLimits` (off by default; at 10 days: approved 15 days ago + nothing received → Cancelled with reason; 4 of 10 received → Closed, 6 short-closed, reason AUTO-EXPIRY; approved 3 days ago though created 20 days ago → kept; no approval row + created 20 days ago → cancelled; Draft 30 days → kept; fully received → kept Approved; sending the cancelled PO → PURCHA-0621; receipt on the closed PO → PURCHA-0489; second run changes nothing; return window 30: accepted 40 days after receipt → PURCHA-0620, rejected → allowed, 5 days → allowed, 0 → no limit) with `TestPOPartialReceiptRules`, `TestPOReceiptPolicyStage7324`, `TestPurchaseReturn*`, and the internal/server catalog / settings / PO tests. **Live** (scratch :8177 built from a %TEMP% mirror of the tree - the live tree briefly did not compile from another session's in-progress WMS files; local DB 5490): both settings under **Configuration → Procurement** with their text, saved 10 / 30; a purchase return of accepted goods on a GRN dated 40 days back → 422 PURCHA-0620 with the catalog message and action, rejected goods → saved Draft; an auto-cancelled PO row shows the stamp and reason with only Print / Delete; sending it → refused. Fixtures (`DLTEST-*`, draft `PRT/HO/26-27/00011`) deleted and both settings rows removed after. One console 404 in the first run did not reproduce on two reruns of the same pages and actions.
- [ ] **78.3 The rest of the day-limit set** `[needs design decision: which of these to build]` - what established ERPs do (researched 2026-10-17), each to default 0 = off:
  - (a) **Expected delivery date + vendor reminders** - SAP: up to three reminder / urging-letter levels in days relative to the delivery date (negative = before it), defaulted from the purchasing info record; Odoo: remind the vendor X days before the receipt date. Needs an *Expected delivery* field on the PO (71.6 lists it as not held).
  - (b) **Late / early receipt check** - SAP: GR posting date checked against the PO delivery date (warning or error), plus a *latest GR date* per line. Ours: receive only within N days after the expected date, or warn on early receipt.
  - (c) **Backdated / forward-dated entry limit** - Business Central: *Allow posting from / to* on the ledger setup with per-user overrides (date formulas since 2026 wave 1). Ours: PO / GRN / transfer / return dates at most N days back (and M ahead), with a role that may exceed it.
  - (d) **GRN not billed after N days** - SAP has no built-in reminder (ME2L lists open POs with no invoice); a flag / report of receipts with no vendor invoice N days after posting.
  - (e) **Purchase requisition expiry** - a requisition not turned into a PO within N days closes.
  - (f) **Quotation validity default** - RFQ quotes already carry `valid_until`; default it to N days from receipt.
  - (g) **Transfer in-transit limit** - flag a dispatched transfer not received within N days.
  - (h) **GST statutory deadline** - input credit and debit / credit notes against a financial year close on 30 November after it (Section 16(4)); a warning when a vendor invoice / note is entered after that - a fixed date, not a day count.
  - *Status 2026-10-18:* list presented to the user; the user asked only to log it as pending - **nothing built, do not start until the user picks**. Suggested order if asked: (c) backdating limit, (d) GRN not billed, (a) expected delivery + reminders ((b) needs (a)'s delivery date).
- [ ] **78.4 Release of this session's work** (71.8 prints, `TestPOPartialReceiptRules`, 78.1, 78.2) - pending, nothing committed or deployed (no push until the user says):
  - commit only the reviewed files; `engines/po_receipt_policy.go` is another session's uncommitted Stage 73.2.4 file that now also carries the `shortClosePurchaseOrder` split - commit it together with that session's work;
  - `go run ./cmd/gendocs` for ERROR_CODES (PURCHA-0620, PURCHA-0621);
  - `pwsh docs/brain/update-brain.ps1` - `brain.map.json` already claims `po_auto_cancel.go` / `po_receipt_policy.go`, but the redraw stops on 27 other sessions' unclaimed files;
  - deploy: binary + `public/` together (no migration); both day settings ship at 0 = off.

## Stage 81 — Product attributes per client from the industry attribute library (opened + built 2026-10-18 from the user's message and attribute-master workbook)

User, 2026-10-18: *"I will create variant for the client and it will be set for the client but disablable by client if needed. won't ask in po or any print out or template or pims or anywhere. and it will stay forever for them. Like for jewllery color,polish,size,stone color. I will share a master with predefined value. All attribute value deletable and attributes disableable."* + `ERP_Industry_Product_Attribute_Master.xlsx` (draft: 10 industries, 53 shared + 618 industry attributes, 192 value sets / 1,569 values, Variant Driver and Value Scope per attribute) + *"See how much erp should have and PIMS. Add accordingly."*

- [x] **81.1 The library in the binary.** `cmd/genattrlib` (stdlib only: an .xlsx is zip + XML) reads the workbook's canonical sheets (03_All_Attributes, 04_Value_Set_Master, plus 01_Industry_Index) by header name into `engines/attribute_library.json` (~363 KB; stripped binary 20.0 / 25 MiB); `engines/attribute_library.go` embeds it like `hsn_master.json`. Rerun the command after editing the workbook. **ERP vs PIM** comes from the workbook's own columns (`LibraryAttributeUse`): Variant Driver *Yes* → variant (ticked); *Category dependent* → variant candidate (listed, not ticked: polish = Surface Finish, Stone Colour, plating, clasp…); otherwise → product information (PIM); Lot / Serial scope → not copied (batch / serial level); 19 shared CORE attributes the Item or GRN already holds (GTIN, HSN, shelf life, weight, traceability, batch / expiry…) → not copied (`libraryCoveredByItem`).
- [x] **81.2 The client's attributes are ProductAttributeDef records.** `db/migrations_stage81_1_attribute_library.sql` adds `used_for` (Variant / Product information; blank on existing ones = offered everywhere as before), `uom`, `library_id`. `ApplyAttributeLibrary` (one transaction) creates the picked attributes with code = library id, label, value type, the value list, its library group as a ProductAttributeGroup (`LIB-<IND>-<GROUP>`), `added_by`; `created_by` is the client's `system` user, else its Super Admin (the operator is not a user of the client - found by the test: `documents.created_by` is an FK). An attribute the client already has (same library id or code) is **skipped and left exactly as the client keeps it** - "stay forever".
- [x] **81.3 Operator screen.** Control Panel → row **Attributes** (`public/view-attribute-library.js`, lazy): industry (the client's own, else Jewellery), Variant / Product information / Not added lists with values, the workbook note, *Has it* / *Similar: …* (a client attribute with a similar name is not ticked), per-row **Use as**, Tick / Untick all, **Add to <client>**. `GET/POST /api/v1/platform/tenants/{tenant}/attribute-library`, platform operators only (a client's own admin gets 403).
- [x] **81.4 "Won't ask anywhere".** Once a client has library attributes its list is closed: the Item form's variant options offer only Variant attributes and no **Other…**; the PO design panel lists only Variant attributes (`GetDesignMatrix` drops Product information), shows no **+ Add attribute** (`attributes_closed`), and `AddDesignAttributeValue` refuses a new attribute (`PIM-0691`) while still accepting a new value. Prints already print values only (`StickerVariantText`).
- [x] **81.5 "Values deletable, attributes disableable".** The Attribute Definition form shows values as chips (× / Add, still the same comma list) for Select attributes; **Status** Inactive switches one off. Deleting a library attribute is refused (`PIM-0690`, `CheckAttributeDelete` in the generic DELETE); `KeepLibraryAttributeIdentity` keeps its code and library id through an edit (clearing library_id would lift the guard; a new code would orphan the stored key). Items keep values that were removed.
- [ ] **81.6 Jewellery profile overlap** **[needs design decision: user]**: the JEWELRY industry profile also puts free-text **Color / Polish / Size / Stone Type** (and Select Metal Type / Purity) fields on Item, which `PrepareItemVariantCode` merges into variant codes under lowercase field-name keys - a second key namespace beside the library's attribute codes. Left untouched (a live jewellery client holds data in them). Options: keep both; hide those fields once a client has library variant attributes (data kept); or map them onto the library attributes. Not built.
- [ ] **81.7 Later, if wanted** (from the workbook's own model, not asked for): per-category attribute rules (05_Category_Rules, 2,166 rows - requiredness per category), multi-select values (5 MultiEnum attributes are single Select today), units per value (G07), approval of regulated values (G01). Not built.
- **Verification (2026-10-18):** `go test ./engines/` `TestAttributeLibraryLoadsAndClassifies`, `TestApplyAttributeLibraryAddsOnceAndKeepsWhatTheClientHas` (in a rolled-back transaction: 3 added + CORE_GTIN skipped; the client's Inactive status and trimmed values survive a second apply; unknown id refused); PO-design / item-name / PIM engine tests and the server route / catalog / control-panel tests pass; `go vet`; `node --check`. Chrome on scratch :8216 against a scratch client `attrlib81` provisioned with `tenantctl` and **purged afterwards** (verify: CLEAN): operator added 78 (9 variant + polish + stone colour, 67 PIM); PO matrix closed with the 11 variant attributes; PO new attribute → 422 PIM-0691; delete → 422 PIM-0690; Item form 11 options, no Other…, no PIM ones; attribute form chips −Silver +Champagne, Inactive saved, code / library id read-only; re-apply skipped and kept the client's changes; no page errors. ## Stage 83 — The top of the screen: no role on the chip; a working-store switcher proposed (opened 2026-10-18 from the user's PO and POS screenshots)

User, 2026-10-18: *"No need to show superadmin in top. As it will be shown in profile. & Lets wire store selection to Top. This 2nd one think & tell me good or bad idea."*

- [x] **83.1 Role off the transaction chip.** `txnContext()` (`public/txn-screen.js`, the one chip every transaction bar uses - PO, GRN, POS, returns, transfers, journals, finance notes) now shows *user · store*; the role stays in the chip's hover text and the profile menu. Verified by rendering the chip (`admin · HO`, title "Signed in as admin (Super Admin)"); `node --check`.
- [ ] **83.2 Working-store switcher in the top bar** **[needs design decision: user - recommended, not built]**. Today a user has exactly one store (`users.location_code`), signed into the session token; the server reads it (`Resolved-Location`) in ~19 places, store-wise document numbering (76.B) among them, and nothing records which other stores a user may work at - so an HO admin billing at Jayanagar sees *HO* on the chip while the POS store box says Jayanagar. Recommended shape: (a) a per-user list of stores they may work at (Super Admin: all; others: their own unless an admin adds more); (b) the switcher shown only to users with more than one store, a plain store name otherwise; (c) switching re-issues the session for that store on the server after checking the list - never a browser-only value, or numbering and scoping would disagree with what the screen says; (d) refused while a POS till session is open at the current store; (e) it sets the default store of new documents and the default filter of lists / reports (with *All stores* for multi-store users) - every form keeps its own visible Store field; (f) the chip and the top bar both show it. Decisions needed: who may switch, and whether lists filter by it.
